We present Baobab, which compiles a $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ ontology with a finite ABox into a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD): it saturates a propositional core under a consequence-based calculus and instantiates the remaining $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ features (nominals, number restrictions, and the role axioms) over the active domain. The SDD's evidence-conditioned weighted model count then trains a perception network to recognize real images under partial ABox supervision: on an ontology that exercises every distinctive $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ feature, a CNN learns to read MNIST digits coupled by a successor relation and recovers latent ontology concepts that an independent perception leaves at chance. When the supervision admits several ontology-consistent completions, an independent perception collapses onto one, a reasoning shortcut: we show that a mixture indexed by the query's justifications can represent the calibrated posterior no independent perception can, and that seeding it from the circuit's enumerated completions attains the Bayes-optimal posterior on a real-image MNIST task where single-WMC and learned mixtures (the BEARS-ensemble hypothesis class) do not: to our knowledge the first to characterize and mitigate reasoning shortcuts in a non-Horn description logic. Soundness of the compiler and the representation result are machine-checked in Lean 4. Code is available at https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/baobab.

</details>


### [190] [The Curious Case of Exploding DecPOMDPs: Containing the Fire through Policy Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17749)
*Nazlı Nur Karabulut,tanya Braun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的DecPOMDP求解视角：从"对智能体计数"转向"对策略计数"，定义了策略计数型DecPOMDP（policy-counted DecPOMDP），并基于紧致表示设计了策略计数型动态规划算法，从而在智能体数量上实现可处理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DecPOMDP作为多智能体不确定性决策的通用框架，其复杂度随智能体数量呈指数级增长。已有的对称性分组（按智能体计数）方法虽可降低模型复杂度和评估代价至多项式依赖，但策略空间仍然爆炸式增长，成为新的瓶颈。

Method: 本文将计数思想从智能体维度迁移到策略维度，定义策略计数型DecPOMDP（policy-counted DecPOMDP），利用策略层面的紧致表示使得策略搜索在智能体数量上具备可处理性，并据此提出策略计数型动态规划（policy-counted dynamic programming）算法进行高效求解。

Result: 通过从计数智能体转向计数策略，在策略计数型DecPOMDP中实现了对智能体数量的可处理性，并利用紧致表示结合动态规划方法高效求解该问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维多智能体DecPOMDP的求解提供了新的可行路径，即通过策略计数而非智能体计数来突破智能体数量的指数复杂度瓶颈。

Abstract: Decentralised partially observable Markov decision processes (DecPOMDPs) provide a general framework for modelling multi-agent decision making under uncertainty. However, DecPOMDPs are known to suffer from exponential complexity in the number of agents. One way to combat this intractability in agent numbers is to look at partitions of agents that exhibit a form of symmetry among agents, allowing for a compact encoding by counting. However, a challenge arises as the policy space explodes, even though the model complexity and evaluation cost reduce to a polynomial dependence. In this paper, we redirect our focus from counting agents to counting policies, which actually enables tractability in agent numbers for so called policy-counted DecPOMDPs. Further, we present policy-counted dynamic programming using the compact representation to solve policy-counted DecPOMDPs efficiently.

</details>


### [191] [D$^2$ACCI: A Dual-Loop Diagnostic Protocol for Evidence-Preserving Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17756)
*Xule Liu,Yijun Liu,Chao Li,Shao Kun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出D²ACCI双循环协议，用于LLM智能体持久记忆系统的诊断驱动迭代开发，并引入DCR分级可观测性指标和D²ACCI-Eval可复用评估工件，在三个公共基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的持久记忆系统涉及多阶段流水线（摄取、检索、过滤、生成），端到端评估只能发现错误发生，却无法定位是哪个阶段导致失败。现有评估通常仅报告聚合性能，缺乏配对统计比较、切片级非回归检查和阶段级诊断轨迹，使得记忆系统迭代难以定位和验证改进。

Method: 提出D²ACCI双循环协议：外层为诊断门控（基于配对证据、受保护切片监控和轨迹级可定位性来提升、功能标记或拒绝记忆干预）；内层为闭环受控迭代。同时引入DCR（分级可观测性指标）衡量失败是否保持可定位性，以及D²ACCI-Eval可重用工件用于门控重放。将该协议实例化为MemStack系统。

Result: 在LoCoMo达到93.59%，LongMemEval达到90.93%，PersonaMem-V2达到57.20%。五项配对消融实验显示补充提取、会话记忆检索和Forget Guard带来了统计显著增益（+1.9至+3.7个百分点，所有p≤.003）。BM25/RRF被保留为受监控的功能标记。诊断审计显示富化的轨迹显著提升了根因一致性，诊断工件达到98-100%的DCR@3，而仅结果日志为0%。

Conclusion: 稳健的记忆系统迭代需要可追踪、统计有依据且回归感知的证据，D²ACCI正好填补了这一空白，验证了诊断驱动的协议能更有效地进行记忆系统开发。

Abstract: Memory is a key capability of LLM agents. Persistent memory extends this across sessions---enabling recall, revision, and personalization. Yet its multi-stage pipeline (ingestion, retrieval, filtering, generation) makes failures difficult to localize: end-to-end evaluation reveals that an error occurred, but not which stage caused it. Existing evaluations often report aggregate performance without paired statistical comparisons, slice-level non-regression checks, or stage-level diagnostic traces. We propose D$^2$ACCI (Diagnostic-Driven Artifact-based Closed-loop Controlled Iteration), a dual-loop protocol whose outer diagnostic gate promotes, feature-flags, or rejects memory interventions based on paired evidence, protected-slice monitoring, and trace-level localizability. We further introduce DCR, a graded observability metric that measures whether failures remain localizable, and D$^2$ACCI-Eval, a reusable artifact for gate replay. We instantiate the protocol in MemStack and evaluate on three public benchmarks, achieving 93.59% on LoCoMo, 90.93% on LongMemEval, and 57.20% on PersonaMem-V2. Five paired ablations show that supplement extraction, session-memory retrieval, and Forget Guard yield statistically significant gains (+1.9 to +3.7pp, all p $\le$ .003). In contrast, BM25/RRF is retained as a monitored feature flag---a distinction invisible to aggregate-only evaluation. A diagnostic audit shows enriched traces substantially improve root-cause agreement over result-only relabeling. Diagnostic artifacts reach 98--100% DCR@3 versus 0% for results-only logs. These results establish that robust memory-system iteration demands traceable, statistically grounded, and regression-aware evidence---exactly the gap D$^2$ACCI fills.

</details>


### [192] [Grounding Healthcare LLMs in a Causal Knowledge Graph: Framework, Metrics, and a Cardiovascular Pilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15382)
*Ummara Mumtaz,Aimen Noor,Awais Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于因果知识图谱的、可复现的评估框架，用于评测大语言模型在医疗干预决策中的推理行为，并在心血管场景中进行了压力测试。框架包含领域因果知识图谱、场景条件子图提取、四种受控接地条件（C1-C4）和基于断言标识符的自动化评分管道。实验显示，集成接地（C4）在因果边、不良事件、证据准确性等指标上表现最优，而未经接地的C1虽原始干预准确性最高但缺乏因果和证据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在医疗决策支持中的评估仍停留在单一答案准确性层面，未能充分评测模型对干预措施、作用机制、不良反应、证据和不确定性的推理能力，缺乏可复现的、面向干预行为的评估框架。

Method: 提出四组件评估框架：(i) 以断言为一等公民节点（保留来源、稳定标识符）的领域因果知识图谱；(ii) 针对临床场景检索相关具体化断言子图的场景条件子图提取；(iii) 四种受控接地条件（未接地C1、知识图谱C2、因果图谱C3、集成C4），改变检索子图在上下文中的组织方式；(iv) 锚定于断言标识符的自动化评分管道，单次前向传播计算干预准确性及其他评估指标。同时构建了覆盖八种推理失败模式的类别平衡场景生成器，并在心血管图谱上实例化。

Result: 指标面板沿可解释、非冗余的轴区分了各条件：C4在因果边F1（0.838）、不良事件F1（0.833）、证据准确性（0.738）和无支撑声明率（0.114）上表现最强；C1原始干预准确性最高（0.948），但无可测量的因果或证据接地。

Conclusion: 该评估框架能够有效区分不同接地条件下的LLM医疗推理行为，揭示了\单答案准确性\与\因果推理和证据接地\之间的关键差距，为医疗领域LLM评估提供了更全面的方法论基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for healthcare decision support, but their evaluations still reward single-answer accuracy rather than reasoning about interventions, mechanisms, harms, evidence, and uncertainty. We propose a reproducible, graph-centered evaluation framework for intervention-oriented LLM behavior in healthcare and stress-test it in a cardiovascular pilot. The framework has four components: (i) a domain causal knowledge graph in which assertions are first-class, provenance-preserving nodes with stable identifiers; (ii) a scenario-conditioned subgraph extraction step that, given any clinical scenario, retrieves the relevant reified-assertion subgraph; (iii) four controlled grounding conditions that vary how the retrieved subgraph is composed into the model's context (ungrounded C1, knowledge-graph C2, causal-graph C3, integrated C4); and (iv) an automated scoring pipeline, anchored on assertion identifiers, that computes intervention accuracy, and other evaluation measures on a single pass. To test the framework, we built a category-balanced scenario generator across eight reasoning failure modes and instantiated it on a cardiovascular graph. The metric panel discriminates conditions along interpretable, non-redundant axes: C4 obtains the strongest causal edge F1 (0.838), adverse-effect F1 (0.833), evidence accuracy (0.738), and unsupported claim rate (0.114), while C1 obtains the highest raw intervention accuracy (0.948) with no measurable causal or evidential grounding.

</details>


### [193] [StartupBench: Benchmarking General-Purpose Agents on Market-Validated End-to-End Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17800)
*Liya Zhu,Xin Ma,Tao Liu,Haodong Wang,Ge Zhang,Jingzhe Ding,Qingshui Gu,Yongjie Zhong,Jinxiang Meng,Yuan Gao,Yunqiu Zhou,Hao Zhu,Jifeng He,Yongzhi Liao,Xinyi Zhang,Chaoxin Li,Yi Zhu,Xi Lin,Duju Zeng,Xiang Gao,Wen Zhang,Yunyang Wang,Duo Wang,Huan Zhou,Zuo Wang,Jin Chen,Kaiyuan Zhang,Chuqian Yu,Tianhao Yu,Longxiang Liu,Jianbo Xue,Huimin Che,Jiahao Wang,Yujia Qin,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Xiaolong Chang,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StartupBench，一个基于市场验证的AI初创公司产品构建的端到端Agent基准测试，用于评估LLM Agent在真实世界用户任务上的表现。实验发现即使最强模型也仅能完成约30%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM和Agent基准测试大多依赖研究者自行选择的任务，无法反映真实用户对AI系统的实际需求，因此难以判断模型能力的进步是否能真正延伸到现实世界的用户工作中。

Method: 系统性地研究具有实际采用证据的AI初创公司产品及其工作流程和用户群体，从中识别AI在各专业领域已有实际需求的真实任务；将这些工作流转化为完整的、面向交付物的任务，并使用细粒度评分标准进行评估；在统一的Agent运行框架下测试多个代表性模型。

Result: 即使最强模型也仅能成功完成约30%的StartupBench任务，尽管在许多任务上取得了实质性部分进展。进一步分析识别出复杂指令遵循和领域专业知识是失败的主要来源。

Conclusion: 许多市场验证的真实工作流仍超出当前通用Agent的可靠能力范围，StartupBench可作为衡量Agent在端到端完成真实用户任务方面进展的经验性度量标准。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models(LLMs) and agents have substantially improved the ability of AI systems to execute complex tasks. Yet existing benchmarks largely rely on researcher-selected tasks, leaving uncertain whether such progress extends to the work that real-world users actually demand from AI systems. We introduce \textbf{StartupBench}, an E2E agent benchmark grounded in market-validated AI startup products. Rather than defining tasks from pre-defined assumptions about useful agent capabilities, we systematically study AI products with demonstrated adoption, together with their product workflows and users, to identify real-world tasks for which AI has established practical demand across diverse professional domains. We translate these workflows into complete deliverable-oriented tasks and evaluate them with fine-grained rubrics capturing their complex requirements. Across representative models evaluated under a unified agent harness, even the strongest model successfully completes only approximately 30\% of StartupBench, despite making substantial partial progress on many tasks. Further analysis identifies aspects like complex instruction following and domain-specific expertise as major sources of failure. Our results reveal that many market-validated workflows remain beyond the reliable capabilities of current general-purpose agents, establishing StartupBench as an empirical measure of progress toward E2E completions of real-world user tasks.

</details>


### [194] [ARASH: Adaptive Retrieval And Shot Selection for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17856)
*Samirasadat Jamalidinan,Yue Xu,Kazem Cheshmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARASH是一种自适应、查询特定的检索与示例选择方法，通过基于训练集局部邻域分析选择最优的few-shot提示，将TabPFN的提示长度和内存使用分别减少1261.5倍和2.56倍，同时保持相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格预测是众多应用中的关键任务。虽然基于大型语言模型改编的表格基础模型（如TabPFN）表现优异，但它们需要大量计算资源且频繁重新训练不切实际。少样本提示作为资源高效的替代方案，但如何为表格数据识别最相关的示例行仍是一个挑战。

Method: ARASH（Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection）方法，通过在训练集内进行局部邻域分析，为每个查询自适应地选择最优示例（shots），以提升表格基础模型的推理效率。

Result: ARASH将TabPFN的提示长度和内存使用分别减少1261.5倍和2.56倍，同时保持与原始方法相当的准确率。

Conclusion: ARASH通过自适应、查询特定的检索和示例选择策略，有效解决了表格数据少样本提示中示例选择困难的问题，在显著降低计算资源消耗的同时保持了模型精度。

Abstract: Tabular prediction is a critical task across numerous applications. The recent success of large language models has sparked various approaches for adapting them to the tabular domain. A prevalent strategy involves training or fine-tuning specialized Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) such as TabPFN. However, TFMs require substantial computational resources, and frequent model retraining is often impractical. In-context learning (ICL), specifically, few-shot prompting, offers a resource-efficient alternative to enhance performance. Yet, identifying the most relevant rows to serve as shots remains a challenge for tabular data. This paper introduces ARASH (Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection), a method that improves TFM efficiency by selecting optimal shots based on local neighborhood analysis within the training set. Our results demonstrate that ARASH reduces the prompt length and memory usage of TabPFN by 1261.5$\times$ and 2.56$\times$, respectively, while providing comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [195] [AutoResearch: Insight In, Hallucination Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17906)
*Yiming Ren,Xiang Liu,Qumeng Sun,Xiao Zhang,Jiahao Li,Haoyang Zhang,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoResearch，一个两阶段自主研究系统，将想法生成（Idea Generation）与想法执行（Idea Execution）相连接，确保研究流程在科学上保持严谨，通过多模型生成与交叉审查产生有根据、可测试的研究计划，并通过独立循证审查来接受研究结论，最终实现"有洞察力输入，无幻觉输出"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究系统虽能执行长研究流程，但单纯自动化并不能保证过程科学严谨。现有系统缺乏对研究想法如何形成以及如何通过实验可靠确立的双重保证，容易产生未经充分验证的研究结论或幻觉。

Method: AutoResearch包含两大阶段：（1）Idea Generation阶段：持续整合新兴研究信号与累积领域知识，识别可迁移的机制性洞察，采用多模型生成与交叉审查产生有根据、可测试的研究计划；（2）Idea Execution阶段：协调智能体将计划分解为实验，迭代实现与诊断，并在接受研究结论前进行独立循证审查。

Result: 在跨模态检索、系统优化和基准驱动的机器学习等多个代表性场景中，AutoResearch将生成的想法转化为可测量的进展，检测并纠正不可靠的实验结果，并基于证据做出继续、修订或终止研究方向的决策。例如在RSICD基准上，AutoResearch生成的想法将平均Recall从32.84提升至34.69，且仅记录了5个审计确认的问题事件，而其他自主研究系统为11-27个。

Conclusion: AutoResearch证明了一种在实验前对有意义洞察进行验证、在接受前对结论进行验证的研究过程范式，即"洞察输入，幻觉输出"（Insight In, Hallucination Out），展示了如何将科学严谨性融入自主研究系统。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems are increasingly capable of executing long research workflows, yet automation alone does not ensure that the resulting process remains scientifically grounded. We introduce AutoResearch, a two-stage system that connects Idea Generation with Idea Execution to address both how research ideas are formed and how they are reliably established through experimentation. In Idea Generation, AutoResearch continuously integrates emerging research signals with accumulated domain knowledge, identifies transferable mechanistic insights, and uses multi-model generation and cross-review to produce grounded, testable research plans. In Idea Execution, coordinated agents decompose these plans into experiments, iteratively implement and diagnose them, and employ independent evidence-based review before accepting research conclusions. Across representative settings in cross-modal retrieval, systems optimization, and benchmark-driven machine learning, AutoResearch turns generated ideas into measurable progress, detects and corrects unreliable experimental results, and makes evidence-conditioned decisions to continue, revise, or terminate research directions. For example, on RSICD benchmark, an AutoResearch-generated idea improves mean Recall from 32.84 to 34.69, while recording only 5 audit-confirmed issue events compared with 11-27 for other autonomous research systems. These results demonstrate a research process in which meaningful insight is grounded before experimentation and conclusions are grounded before acceptance: Insight In, Hallucination Out.

</details>


### [196] [Adaptive Policy Portfolios for Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17929)
*Kasper Engelen,Sebastian Junges,Guillermo A. Pérez,Marnix Suilen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）中的自适应策略组合（adaptive policy portfolios）问题：离线合成有限的记忆less随机策略集合并配合轻量级在线选择器，以应对未知但固定的动态环境。作者给出了组合认证和合成的复杂度理论结果（认证为∀ℝ-完备，合成为∃∀ℝ-完备），并提出了一个支持运行时专业化的离线组合构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的鲁棒MDP方法对一组合理的转移函数优化单一策略，但当事后实际动态是固定且可部分辨识时，这种"一刀切"策略会过于保守。自适应策略组合通过离线合成多个策略并在线选择最优者，可以在不牺牲鲁棒性的前提下降低保守性，因此需要理解这种策略组合的认证与合成问题的理论复杂度界限。

Method: 本文从计算复杂性理论角度研究策略组合的核心问题：(1) 组合认证问题——给定策略组合，判定其在各环境下是否达到鲁棒遗憾目标，证明即使对于确定性策略和非循环RMDP也是∀ℝ-完备的；(2) 组合合成问题——合成一元有界大小的组合，证明对一般有理多面体是∃∀ℝ-完备的（即使固定折扣和非循环动态）；(3) 提出一种离线组合构造方法，支持运行时专业化以在线适配具体环境。

Result: 证明了：(1) 给定组合的认证在非循环(s,a)-矩形RMDP中即使对确定性策略也是∀ℝ-完备的；(2) 合成一元有界大小组合对一般有理多面体是∃∀ℝ-完备的，即使固定折扣和非循环动态；(3) 单策略情形在组合和代数层面都已经是困难的。此外给出了支持运行时专业化的离线组合构造方法。

Conclusion: 自适应策略组合的认证与合成问题在计算上是高度困难的（分属∀ℝ与∃∀ℝ完备类），表明这类问题的内在计算壁垒远超一般NP类。与Ghavamzadeh等人(2016)专注于近似与松弛的研究路径不同，本文给出了精确的复杂度刻画，并指出了实用的离线构造方向。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes optimize one policy against a set of plausible transition functions. This can be conservative when the unknown dynamics are fixed and become partially identifiable after deployment. We study adaptive policy portfolios: finite sets of memoryless randomized policies synthesized offline and paired with a lightweight online selector. Robust regret is a natural measure of portfolio quality: for each plausible environment, it measures the loss of the best portfolio member relative to the policy that would have been optimal had that environment been known. Related regret objectives were studied by Ghavamzadeh et al. (2016) with an emphasis on approximations and relaxations for safe policy improvement. We give a complexity-theoretic account of portfolio certification and synthesis. Certifying a given portfolio is $\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete already for deterministic portfolios in acyclic (s,a)-rectangular RMDPs. Synthesizing a portfolio of unary-bounded size is $\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete for general rational polytopes, even with fixed discount and acyclic dynamics. The single-policy case is already hard, both combinatorially and algebraically. Finally, we present an offline portfolio construction that is amenable to runtime specialization.

</details>


### [197] [EvoTS-Agent: A Self-Evolving LLM Agent for Financial Time Series Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17933)
*Lei Jiang,Ye Wei,Xinyu Xi,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Yifan Bao,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoTS-Agent，一种验证引导的自进化LLM智能体，用于金融时间序列变点检测，通过三种互补算子（Revision、Alternative Strategy、Recombination）自动演化实验轨迹并适配不同数据集。实验表明其在四个基准数据集上优于现有基于LLM的智能体，且在多个主干LLM上保持100%执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳性和异质性统计特征，单一无监督算法无法在跨资产和市场制度下表现一致。传统工作流依赖专家进行模型选择、特征设计和超参数调优，限制了可扩展性和适应性。

Method: EvoTS-Agent通过三个阶段工作：(1) 精心设计探索性数据分析以刻画数据集特征并初始化候选检测模型；(2) 通过三种互补算子演化可执行的实验轨迹——Revision利用当前最优解，Alternative Strategy在进展停滞时探索不同建模方向，Recombination综合高性能轨迹中的互补证据；(3) 验证反馈全程引导轨迹演化，使检测流程自适应各数据集的统计特性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，EvoTS-Agent一致优于现有基于LLM的智能体，并且在所有评估的主干LLM上均保持100%的执行成功率。

Conclusion: EvoTS-Agent通过验证引导的自我进化机制，实现了无需大量人工干预的自适应金融时间序列变点检测，在性能稳健性和执行可靠性方面均表现出优势。

Abstract: Financial time series exhibit non-stationary and heterogeneous statistical properties, making change-point detection challenging because no single unsupervised algorithm performs consistently across assets and market regimes. Conventional workflows consequently depend heavily on expert-driven model selection, feature design, and hyperparameter tuning, limiting their scalability and adaptability. We propose EvoTS-Agent, a validation-guided self-evolving LLM agent for autonomous financial time-series change-point detection. EvoTS-Agent first performs curated exploratory data analysis to characterize dataset properties and initialize candidate detection models. It then evolves executable experiment trajectories through three complementary operators: \textit{Revision} exploits the current best solution, \textit{Alternative Strategy} explores fundamentally different modeling directions when progress stagnates, and \textit{Recombination} synthesizes complementary evidence from high-performing trajectories. Validation feedback guides trajectory evolution throughout the search, enabling the agent to adapt its detection pipeline to the statistical characteristics of each dataset while preserving reliable optimization. Experiments across four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EvoTS-Agent consistently outperforms existing LLM-based agents while maintaining a 100\% execution success rate across all evaluated backbone LLMs.

</details>


### [198] [Procedural Content Metageneration via Program Search and Continual Abstraction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17947)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Continual Abstraction Discovery (CAD)方法，在程序化内容生成的进化搜索中，从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语到辅助模块中。实验在Sokoban、Zelda、Dangerous Dave和Lode Runner四个游戏领域进行，跨越CAD与固定领域API的2x2实验共完成160次完整运行。结果显示CAD在所有八个领域和API组合中均提升了平均最终最佳适应度，且学习到的库被大多数后续程序采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能够生成可执行程序，使得可以直接搜索程序化内容生成器而非单个关卡。为了提升进化程序搜索的效率，研究者希望探索如何发现和复用可重用的程序原语，从而加速进化过程并提高生成内容的质量。

Method: 每个运行通过语言模型变异和交叉操作逐代进化完整的Python生成器。引入CAD（Continual Abstraction Discovery）方法，从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语到一个运行特有的辅助模块中。实验设计为2x2交叉设计：是否使用CAD × 是否提供固定的手写领域API，共四个细胞，每个细胞至少运行10次、每次50代，总计160次完整运行。

Result: CAD在所有八个领域和API比较中均提高了平均最终最佳适应度。在所有使用CAD的运行中，学习到的库被大多数后期程序采纳，并反复重新发现了验证、可达性和结构相关的工具程序。

Conclusion: 发现可复用原语能够提升内容生成器的进化程序搜索效果，验证了CAD方法的有效性及其在不同游戏领域中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable programs, which makes it possible to search directly over procedural content generators rather than individual levels. We study this approach in Sokoban, Zelda, Dangerous Dave, and Lode Runner. Each run evolves complete Python generators through language-model mutation and crossover. We introduce Continual Abstraction Discovery, or CAD, which extracts reusable primitives from high-fitness programs into a run-specific helper module. A 2x2 experiment crosses CAD with access to a fixed hand-written domain API. The completed data set contains 160 complete runs, with at least ten 50-generation runs in every cell. CAD raises mean final best fitness in all eight domain and API comparisons. Across all CAD runs, learned libraries are adopted by most later programs and repeatedly rediscover validation, reachability, and structural utilities. These results support that discovering reusable primitives improves evolutionary program search for content generators.

</details>


### [199] [Towards Zero-Shot Task Transfer with Neurosymbolic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17959)
*Isidoro Tamassia,Lennert De Smet,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种新的神经符号世界模型（neurosymbolic world model）公式，将奖励预测与观测重建解耦，使奖励预测仅依赖于潜在状态中结构化的符号组件，从而支持在相同符号状态空间上对新奖励函数的零样本适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的基于模型的强化学习方法学习神经世界模型，通过在潜在空间中规划来实现策略改进，但这类模型通常是任务相关的：它们学习不可解释的潜在表示，这些表示与训练任务绑定，难以泛化到新任务。因此需要一种方法使世界模型可以跨任务泛化。

Method: 提出一种新颖的世界模型公式：将观测重建与奖励预测解耦，使奖励预测仅依赖于整个潜在状态的一个子集——结构化、符号化的组件。这种神经符号（neurosymbolic）世界模型可以在无需额外环境交互的情况下，对定义在同一符号状态空间上的新奖励函数进行零样本（zero-shot）适应。

Result: 该神经符号世界模型展示了相较于纯神经方法的强泛化属性，在零样本适应新奖励函数方面表现出明显优势。

Conclusion: 解耦奖励预测与观测重建、并利用结构化符号表示，是实现任务无关世界模型的关键，能够显著提升跨任务泛化能力，优于纯神经方法。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods learn neural world models that allow policy improvement by planning in a latent space, without assumptions on the structure of the underlying environment. While expressive, these models are generally task-dependent: they learn uninterpretable latent representations that are tied to the training task and thus hard to generalize to new tasks. In this work, we present a novel world model formulation where the reward prediction only depends on a subset of structured, symbolic components of the whole latent state. Decoupling observation reconstruction and reward prediction allows us to learn world models that can adapt zero-shot, i.e. without further environment interactions, to new reward functions defined over the same symbolic state space. We discuss the main advantages and challenges of learning these neurosymbolic world models and demonstrate the strong generalisation properties of our approach over purely neural methods.

</details>


### [200] [Can Large Language Models Explain Flight Safety Events? A Prior-Guided Semantic LLM-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18017)
*Lu Xu,Xu Li,Linjiang Zheng,Fan Li,Riquan Zhang,Jiaxing Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlightLLM，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的先验引导语义方法，用于可解释的飞行安全分析。该方法通过特征工程解决模态不一致问题、语义离散化模块将数值模式转化为定性描述、引入 CatBoost 作为统计专家注入先验指导、采用对比少样本学习缓解数据稀缺，并设计结构化提示嵌入航空领域知识，在 704 个真实 A320 硬着陆样本上实现了具有竞争力的分类性能与直接合理的因果解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行数据分析不仅要准确检测风险事件，更重要的是在飞行员操控行为层面清晰解释其根本原因。现有可解释 AI 技术（如特征重要性图）往往需要大量领域知识才能转化为操作性解释。LLM 在语言推理方面表现优异，但直接应用于该领域面临模态不一致、分类能力有限、任务特定数据稀缺以及缺乏领域知识等关键挑战。

Method: FlightLLM 包含四个核心组件：(1) 特征工程：结合统计描述符与物理意义的飞行指标，解决模态不一致问题；(2) 语义离散化模块（Semantic Discretization）：将抽象数值模式转换为与语言推理更兼容的定性描述；(3) CatBoost 统计专家：将分类预测结果注入提示作为先验引导，弥补 LLM 分类能力不足；(4) 对比少样本学习策略：缓解数据有限的问题；(5) 结构化提示设计：嵌入航空领域知识到推理过程中。

Result: 以硬着陆（hard landing）这一因果机制复杂的代表性风险事件为锚点，在包含 704 个真实 A320 飞行样本的数据集上评估 FlightLLM。实验结果显示该方法在保持竞争性分类性能的同时，能够生成直接且合理的事件原因解释。

Conclusion: FlightLLM 通过先验引导的语义化 LLM 方法有效解决了 LLM 在飞行安全分析中面临的多个挑战，实现了分类性能与可解释性的良好平衡，为可解释飞行安全分析提供了一种可行且有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Improving flight safety with flight data requires not only accurate detection of risk events, but more importantly, clear interpretation of their underlying causes at the level of pilot control behavior. Existing explainable AI techniques, such as feature importance maps, often require considerable domain knowledge to translate them into operationally meaningful explanations. Large Language Models (LLMs), which excel at language reasoning, bring a promising solution to this issue. However, applying LLMs in this domain presents key challenges such as modal inconsistency, limited classification ability, scarcity of task-specific data for fine-tuning, and lack of domain knowledge. To overcome these challenges, we propose FlightLLM, a prior-guided semantic LLM-based approach for interpretable flight safety analysis. Specifically, we first perform feature engineering to address modal inconsistency, combining statistical descriptors with physically meaningful flight indicators. This representation is further processed by a Semantic Discretization module, which converts abstract numerical patterns into qualitative descriptions that are more compatible with language reasoning. In addition, since LLMs are not inherently strong classifiers, CatBoost is incorporated as a statistical expert, and its prediction results are injected into the prompt as prior guidance. A contrastive few-shot learning strategy is further adopted to compensate for limited data. Finally, we design structured prompts to embed aviation-specific knowledge into the inference process. Using hard landing, a representative risk event with complex causal mechanisms, as an anchor point, we evaluate FlightLLM on a dataset of 704 real-world A320 flight samples. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance while generating direct and reasonable explanations for event causes.

</details>


### [201] [StagedWorkspace: A Versioned Workspace for Knowledge-Work Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18050)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Yifan Zhou,Akshay Kalose,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StagedWorkspace，一个为知识工作AI智能体设计的版本化工作空间，将解析视图、审阅diff和提交工件通过内容哈希绑定到原生文件的特定版本，解决多格式文档（PDF、电子表格、幻灯片、笔记本等）中"视图-文件-版本"不一致的工作空间状态契约问题。实验表明该方法在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents上显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多地执行知识工作（生成和修改代码仓库、文档、电子表格、幻灯片、报告等持久化数字工件），但智能体搜索的解析视图、编辑的原生文件、审阅的变更和提交的工件可能指向同一工作产品的不同版本。编码智能体已通过仓库契约部分解决此问题（搜索、diff和测试均基于同一版本），但PDF、电子表格、幻灯片、笔记本和混合格式项目文件夹中尚缺乏类似明确的契约，导致工作空间状态不一致。

Method: 提出StagedWorkspace版本化工作空间架构：(1) 将解析记录（parsed records）和审阅diff绑定到原生文件的内容哈希上，在文件变化时同步更新；(2) 提供双通道访问（dual parsed/native access），让智能体既能通过解析视图搜索内容，又能直接编辑原生文件；(3) 对编辑操作生成可审阅的diff，并确保提交工件与工作空间状态一致。使用固定测试框架（fixed-harness ablations）在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents基准上评估，并进行了57个文件编辑任务的配对审阅消融实验。

Result: 在固定测试框架消融实验中，双解析/原生访问在所有测试模型上取得最高点估计值；相比单一受限视图，OfficeQA Pass@1提升8.3-12.1个百分点，APEX平均rubric分数提升4.7-9.2个百分点。SW-AGENT在OfficeQA上使用Gemini 3.1 Pro达到63.9%（对比已发表同模型分数29.3%），在APEX上使用GPT-5.4 Nano达到42.1（对比25.5）。57个文件编辑任务的配对消融进一步表明diff可见时观测得分更高。

Conclusion: 研究识别出工作空间状态是知识工作智能体的一个重要实验变量，并激励未来基准测试应将证据评分、分阶段编辑和提交工件视为显式状态转换来衡量，而非仅评估最终结果。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform knowledge work (i.e., produce and modify persistent digital artifacts such as code repositories, documents, spreadsheets, slides, reports), yet the parsed views they search, the native files they edit, the changes they review, and the artifacts they submit can refer to different versions of the same work product. We formulate this as a workspace-state contract: every view should be explicitly tied to a version of the evolving workspace state. Coding agents partly address this need through repository contracts for search, diffs, and tests, whereas an analogous contract is less explicit for PDFs, spreadsheets, slides, notebooks, and mixed-format project folders. We propose StagedWorkspace, a versioned workspace for knowledge-work agents. The workspace binds parsed records and review diffs to content hashes of the native files as they change. In fixed-harness ablations on OfficeQA Pro and APEX-Agents, dual parsed/native access has the highest point estimate for every tested model; relative to the more limiting single view, it improves OfficeQA Pass@1 by 8.3-12.1 points and APEX mean rubric score by 4.7-9.2 points. SW-AGENT scores 63.9% with Gemini 3.1 Pro on OfficeQA and 42.1 with GPT-5.4 Nano on APEX, compared with published same-model scores of 29.3% and 25.5, respectively. A paired review-axis ablation on 57 file-editing tasks further finds higher observed scores when diffs are visible. These results identify workspace state as an experimental variable in knowledge-work agents and motivate benchmarks that score evidence, staged edits, and submitted artifacts as explicit state transitions.

</details>


### [202] [HLSR: Hybrid Live Forecast Selective Dynamic Vehicle Rerouting for Real-Time Congestion Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18056)
*Xiao Wang,Shun Ren Yang,Hui Nien Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HLSR框架，一种选择性混合实时-预测车辆重路由方法，在有限干预范围内融合实时边缘速度与短时预测，以缓解城市交通拥堵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通拥堵降低生产力并增加出行成本与排放；网络级实时最短路径重路由在模拟中高效，但假设每辆车在每个决策周期都被重规划，这在现实中不可行。

Method: HLSR包括：双阈值拥塞检测、校准的上游车辆选择、驾驶员定制化的旅行时间预测，并引入接近车辆扩展、旅行时间加权k最短路径生成，以及基于视界的混合实时-预测段速度用于多成本路径分配。

Result: 论文未在摘要中给出具体定量结果。

Conclusion: HLSR通过有限干预范围实现选择性重路由，兼顾实时数据与预测信息，为解决现实场景中大规模车辆重路由的可行性问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion reduces productivity and increases travel cost and emissions. Network-wide live travel-time shortest-path rerouting can be highly effective in simulation, but assumes that essentially every on-road vehicle is replanned every decision period. We propose HLSR, a selective hybrid live--forecast vehicle rerouting framework that fuses live edge speeds with short-horizon forecasts under limited intervention scope. Building on dual-threshold congestion detection, calibrated upstream selection, and driver-tailored travel-time prediction, HLSR further introduces approaching-vehicle expansion, travel-time-weighted k-shortest-path generation, and a horizon-dependent hybrid live--forecast segment speed used in multi-cost route allocation.

</details>


### [203] [Delegation Asymmetry in Agentic Recommender Systems: Measuring Two-Sided Receptivity in Online Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18058)
*Daria Leshchikova,Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Valerii Klimov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了用户对自主LLM代理（即以用户名义对话的AI代理）的接受度问题，通过对大型约会平台4千余名活跃用户的两项大规模调查，开发了基于潜在变量的代理接受度测量模型，发现发送与接收代理沟通的意愿是高相关但可区分的独立构念，并量化了系统的"委派不对称"（部署自己代理的门槛远低于接受他人代理），进而通过反事实分析评估了互惠要求和按接收接受度路由设计对交互量、参与度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM代理在匹配平台中作为用户的对话助理日益普及，但其长期可行性依赖一个关键且鲜被检验的条件：用户不仅需要接受把对话委托给代理，还必须接受与他人代理进行沟通。现有研究几乎未从"接收方"视角系统考察这一双向接受条件，缺乏对用户代理接受度结构的量化理解与可操作的设计洞见。

Method: ①基于大型约会平台的活跃用户样本进行两项调查（N=2,894研究生成式资料特征；N=2,617研究自主对话代理），以两种语言实施；②开发基于等级反应模型（graded response model）加潜在回归的潜在变量测量模型，用于估计用户的代理接受度；③通过模型比较（ΔBIC）检验发送与接收意愿是否为可分离构念；④以随机配对反事实模拟估计有向二元组（dyad）中"部署+接收"的组合比例；⑤设计反事实分析：比较互惠要求与按接收接受度路由两种机制对交互量和单次接触参与度的影响，并进行保留目标项的样本外验证（AUC 0.88）。

Result: ①发送与接收代理沟通意愿是相关很高（ρ=0.92）但在统计上可分离的构念（ΔBIC=52），且跨语言呈现部分测量不变性；②存在系统性委派不对称：部署自己代理的接受度阈值（-0.38）远低于与对方代理互动（+0.32，完全参与需+1.39），部署意愿均值约为接愿意愿的三倍；③在随机配对反事实下，仅4-13%的有向二元组实现"代理部署+对方接收"的组合，并存在明显的性别方向性失衡；④互惠要求将排除近三分之二潜在部署，使交互量减少一半以上；⑤按接收接受度路由代理联系可使每次接触的参与度提升3倍，该效果在保留目标项的样本外验证中稳健（AUC 0.88，四分位数提升3.1倍）。

Conclusion: 代理沟通的接受度是匹配平台设计必须正视的双向条件——发送与接收是可区分的构念，且存在显著的不对称。研究为agentic推荐系统设计提供了实证基础，建议在披露机制、选择加入（opt-in）设计以及感知接收接受度的匹配路由方面采取差异化策略，以改善代理通信的有效性和用户体验。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents that converse on a user's behalf are an emerging design pattern in matching platforms, yet their viability depends on a condition rarely examined: users must accept not only delegating conversation to an agent, but also receiving agent-mediated communication from others. We study this condition using two large-scale surveys of active users of a major dating platform (N=2,894 on generative profile features; N=2,617 on autonomous conversational agents, fielded in two languages). We develop a latent-variable measurement model of agent receptivity based on graded response models with latent regression, and show via model comparison that willingness to send and willingness to receive agent communication are distinct constructs: highly correlated (rho=0.92) but separable (Delta BIC=52), with partial measurement invariance across languages. The model quantifies a systematic delegation asymmetry: deploying one's own agent requires far lower receptivity (threshold -0.38) than engaging a counterpart's agent (+0.32; full engagement +1.39), and mean deployment propensity exceeds engagement propensity roughly threefold. Under a random-pairing counterfactual derived from stated receptivity, only 4-13% of directed dyads combine agent deployment with receiver engagement, with a pronounced gender-directional imbalance. Design counterfactuals quantify the levers: a reciprocity requirement cuts interaction volume by half or more by excluding nearly two-thirds of would-be deployment, while routing agent contacts on receive receptivity triples per-contact engagement, a lift that survives out-of-sample validation with the target item held out (AUC 0.88, 3.1x quartile lift under respondent-level cross-validation). We discuss implications for agentic recommender design, including disclosure, opt-in mechanics, and receptivity-aware matchmaking.

</details>


### [204] [On the Fragility of Self-Improving Agents: Variance, Task Order, and Underspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18066)
*Qinyuan Ye,Yu Li,Yada Pruksachatkun,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了基于记忆的自我改进智能体，发现其评估噪声大且改进效果高度依赖任务顺序（隐式课程），指出任务与环境欠规定性是脆弱性的成因，并呼吁更严格的评估协议和人类监督机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于记忆的自我改进智能体在近期文献中展现出良好前景，但其可靠性方面被严重忽视。已有研究未系统考察评估方差和任务顺序对智能体性能的影响，导致对其实际稳定性和泛化能力的认知存在盲区。

Method: 对两种基于记忆的方法进行全面重新评估，沿两个维度扩展评估范围：(1) 多次运行以量化方差；(2) 随机打乱任务顺序以研究顺序效应。进一步通过人工检查智能体记忆提出假设，并通过在记忆构建过程中引入详细评分标准和环境反馈等更充分的信息来验证欠规定性假设。

Result: 发现两个关键现象：一是复杂环境和多步任务中智能体评估本身就存在固有噪声，自我改进循环会进一步放大噪声；二是智能体的改进高度依赖任务顺序，默认顺序隐式构建了课程，成为成功的隐藏前提。引入更详细的任务规格信息虽能部分弥补性能退化，但仍有显著差距，表明存在其他未表征的因素。

Conclusion: 呼吁自我改进智能体采用更严格的评估协议（多次运行报告、压力测试），并倡导设计支持有效人类监督的系统和界面，防止智能体以不可预见的方式失败。

Abstract: Memory-based self-improving agents--those that learn from an online stream of tasks and improve over time by maintaining a textual memory bank--have shown great promise in recent literature. However, the reliability aspects of these methods have been critically overlooked. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive re-evaluation of two memory-based methods, broadening the scope of evaluation along two axes: (1) including multiple runs to quantify variance, and (2) randomly shuffling the tasks to investigate the effect of task order. Through these experiments, we make two observations that expose the fragility of current methods: First, agent evaluation is inherently noisy in complex environments and on multi-step tasks, and stacking a self-improving loop on top can further amplify this noise. Second, the agent's improvement is highly dependent on task order. Prior works often adopt default orderings that impose an implicit curriculum, acting as a hidden prerequisite for success.
  To better understand this fragility, we manually examine the agents' memory and hypothesize that task and environment underspecification contribute to this fragility. We validate this hypothesis by incorporating information that enables better specification, such as detailed rubrics and environment feedback, into the memory construction process. While this added information partially closes the performance degradation in previous experiments, significant gaps still remain, suggesting that other uncharacterized factors contribute to this fragility. Looking ahead, our work advocates for more rigorous evaluation protocols for self-improving agents by reporting results across multiple runs and stress-testing them under challenging conditions. Moreover, our findings on underspecification call for systems and interfaces that enable effective human oversight, preventing agents from failing in unforeseeable ways.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Proactive Road Safety Intervention in Australia: Predicting Risky Driving Hotspots from Connected Vehicle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16913)
*Adriana-Simona Mihăiţă,Clarence Cheung,Artur Grigorev,Tuo Mao,David Lillo-Trynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用澳大利亚大悉尼地区的网联车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值（急刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g）量化危险驾驶行为，构建时空热力图识别高危区域，并对八种预测模型（集成学习、深度学习、经典时序方法三大类）进行基准对比，以在地方政府区域（LGA）层面检测和预测近距离碰撞风险驾驶事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道路安全监测是反应式的，依赖事故记录的事后分析，无法在伤亡发生前主动识别高危地点和危险驾驶行为。这一主动预防性挑战尚未得到充分探索，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用网联车辆遥测数据量化危险驾驶（g力阈值），构建时空热力图识别高危区域，然后基准对比三类八种预测模型：集成学习（随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM）、深度学习（LSTM、N-BEATS）、经典时序方法（ARIMA、指数平滑、Prophet），以MAE为评价指标在LGA层面进行预测。

Result: ARIMA取得最低平均绝对误差（MAE: 162.21），与LSTM（MAE: 163.92）表现相当，优于所有集成学习方法；N-BEATS的MAE为180.75。悉尼内城区和西部LGA（CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown）被识别为持续高危区域。

Conclusion: 在训练数据量有限的情况下，精简的时序模型与深度学习方法的预测性能相当甚至更优。研究展示了基于物联网的网联车辆数据在支持主动式道路安全干预方面的潜力，并为高危区域提出了针对性政策行动建议。

Abstract: Road safety monitoring has historically been reactive, relying on crash-record analysis after fatalities and injuries have already occurred. Proactive identification of high-risk locations and dangerous driving behaviour before incidents occur is a critical but underexplored challenge. This paper addresses this gap using connected vehicle telemetry data from Greater Sydney, Australia, to detect and forecast near-miss risky driving events at the Local Government Area (LGA) level. Risky driving is quantified through g-force thresholds (hard braking >0.6g, harsh cornering >0.47g, harsh acceleration >0.5g), and spatio-temporal heatmaps are constructed to identify high-risk zones. Eight predictive models are benchmarked across three families: ensemble learning (Random Forests, XGBoost, LightGBM), deep learning (LSTM, N-BEATS), and classical time-series methods (ARIMA, Exponential Smoothing, Prophet). ARIMA achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE: 162.21), performing comparably to LSTM (MAE: 163.92) and outperforming all ensemble methods, with N-BEATS reaching an MAE of 180.75. These results demonstrate that parsimonious time-series models are competitive with deep learning approaches when training data volume is limited. The study highlights the potential of IoT-based connected vehicle data to support proactive road safety interventions, with Sydney's inner and western LGAs (CBD, Parramatta, Bankstown) identified as persistent high-risk zones warranting targeted policy action.

</details>


### [206] [Detecting and Discriminating Operator Misspecification in Hybrid PDE-Parameter Learning: a Reference-Free Instrument, with Discrimination Bounded In Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16925)
*Eric Fock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种仪器/统计量，可在单次拟合中无需oracle地区分混合PDE-参数估计器假定算子错误与参数不可辨识这两种失败模式。在自伴抛物型反问题上，该信息矩阵统计量在正确设定下中位数为0.19，拒绝率0.033（低于预注册阈值0.10），在两种误设下飙升至224和85且在每次重复中均触发；而在正确设定但不可辨识的设计下保持静默（n=200时0.050，Clopper-Pearson CI [0.024,0.090]），同时秩统计量在预注册边界处坍缩至零。两种读法实现三种设计下的失败分离，这是核心贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精度检验存在盲区：误设估计器的域内RMSE（2.7×10⁻²）低于观测噪声水平（σ≥0.05），而系数在零噪声下已偏差29.7%、在最大噪声下偏差31.2%。需要一种能在单次拟合中诊断估计器设定错误而非仅仅检测到性能下降的方法，将"算子假定错误"与"参数不可辨识"两种失败分离。

Method: 构建基于信息矩阵的统计量，使用plug-in尺度和per-seed参数，从单次拟合中读取估计器假定算子是否正确；同时利用秩统计量在不可辨识设计下检测参数可辨识性。通过三种设计（正确设定、两种误设、正确但不可辨识）进行验证。还通过多架构对比（一参数曲线拟合、裸参数、49/241参数MLP、物理信息网络）排除了失败源于网络架构的可能性。

Result: 在正确设定下统计量表现正常（中位数0.19，拒绝率0.033低于0.10上限）；两种误设下统计量骤升（224和85）且全部重复触发；在不可辨识设计中统计量保持静默（0.050，CI包含0.05）而秩统计量坍缩至预注册边界。所有网络架构收敛到同一pseudo-true（闭式匹配度0.07%），但物理信息网络因复合目标收敛到不相交的解。报告了仪器失效场景和一个预注册阴性结果（神经网络损失于Tikhonov正则化反演）。

Conclusion: 贡献在于将两种失败模式分离（而非仅仅检测），在样本内提供界、样本外提供方向。作者还指出了仪器的盲区、神经网络估计器在恢复任务中表现逊于Tikhonov正则化反演的预注册阴性结果，以及保证成立但训练网络违反该保证的假设条件。

Abstract: We build an instrument that reads, from a single fit and with no oracle, whether the operator a hybrid PDE-parameter estimator postulates is wrong-and separates that from a merely unidentifiable parameter. On one self-adjoint parabolic inverse problem, an information-matrix statistic with plug-in scale and per-seed parameter has median 0.19 under correct specification, rejection rate $0.033$ against a pre-registered ceiling of $0.10$, and rises to $224$ and $85$ under two misspecifications, firing in every replicate. On a correctly specified but non-identifiable design it stays mute-$0.050$ at $n=200$, Clopper-Pearson $[0.024, 0.090]$-while a rank statistic collapses to zero at a pre-registered boundary $c_5^*=2.15\times10^{-3}.$ Two readings of one fit therefore separate the two failures across the three designs a deployable test reaches. That separation is the contribution; detection alone is a crowded flank. In sample it is a bound, out of sample a direction. It is needed because the usual accuracy check is blind: the misspecified estimator's in-domain RMSE is $2.7\times 10^{-2}$, below the observation noise for $σ\geq 0.05,$ while the coefficient is wrong by $29.7\%$ at zero noise, $31.2\%$ at the loudest. Nor is the failure architectural: a one-parameter curve fit, a bare parameter and multilayer perceptrons of $49$ and $241$ parameters converge to the same pseudo-true, matched in closed form to $0.07\%,$ whereas a physics-informed network, with its composite objective, converges to a disjoint one. We report where the instrument is blind, a pre-registered negative where a neural estimator loses to Tikhonov-regularized inversion at recovery, and the hypothesis under which its guarantee holds but a trained network violates it.

</details>


### [207] [Data-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization for Target Model Data Selection in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16926)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Data-DPO，一种面向目标模型的监督微调数据选择方法，通过一步探测观察目标模型的局部训练反馈，将样本间激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型来学习目标模型感知的数据偏好，并结合外部质量分数和边际多样性构建训练子集，在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT上优于现有基线并稳定超越全量数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法通常将数据价值视为相对静态的属性，忽略了数据与目标模型能力分布之间的兼容性。因此需要一种能够感知目标模型特性的数据选择方法。

Method: Data-DPO包含三个关键步骤：（1）通过一步探测观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈；（2）将样本间的激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型学习目标模型感知的数据偏好；（3）在最终选择阶段结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性构建训练子集。

Result: 在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT数据集上的实验表明，Data-DPO在多种数据预算下持续优于现有数据选择基线方法，并稳定超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: Data-DPO通过将目标模型的动态学习状态纳入数据选择过程，实现了更稳定和更有效的数据子集构建，验证了数据与目标模型能力分布兼容性在数据选择中的重要性。

Abstract: Data selection in supervised fine-tuning aims to select a small set of effective samples from large-scale candidate data, reducing training cost while preserving model performance. However, existing methods usually treat data value as a relatively static property, and pay limited attention to the compatibility between data and the capability distribution of the target model. To address this issue, we propose Data-DPO, a target model-oriented SFT data selection method. Data-DPO observes the local training feedback of the target model on different samples through one-step probing, transforms activation differences among samples into pairwise data preferences, and trains a lightweight reward model to learn target-model-aware data preferences. In the final selection stage, Data-DPO further combines target model preference, external quality scores, and marginal diversity to construct a more stable and effective training subset. Experimental results on Vision-Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that Data-DPO consistently outperforms existing data selection baselines under multiple data budgets and stably surpasses full data training performance.

</details>


### [208] [Hierarchical Data Selection via Manifold Coverage and Sparse Feature Coverage in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16927)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MASS方法，将数据选择形式化为从粗到细的分层覆盖问题，通过密集自编码器学习低维主流形坐标进行粗粒度语义分组，再在每组内使用TopK稀疏自编码器进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT上超越多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着监督微调数据量的持续增长，如何从大规模候选池中筛选高质量子集以降低训练成本并提升模型性能至关重要。现有方法直接在原始嵌入空间中度量多样性，但几何指标会混淆主导语义方向、细粒度监督差异和局部噪声，导致数据选择效果受限。

Method: MASS将数据选择建模为粗到细的分层覆盖问题：首先利用密集自编码器学习低维主流形坐标，实现粗粒度的语义分组；随后在每个组内使用TopK稀疏自编码器进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，从而在保留质量信息的同时实现高覆盖的数据选择。

Result: 在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT两个基准上，MASS在多种数据预算下一致优于强数据选择基线，并且在若干设置中仅使用少量子集数据即可匹配甚至超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: MASS通过解耦嵌入空间中的不同信号（主导语义、类别差异、噪声），采用分层覆盖策略有效提升了数据选择的效率与效果，证明了粗到细分层方法在大规模数据筛选中的优越性。

Abstract: As supervised fine-tuning data continues to scale, selecting high-value subsets from large candidate pools is crucial for reducing training cost and improving model performance. Existing methods often measure diversity directly in the original embedding space, where geometric metrics entangle dominant semantic directions, fine-grained supervision differences, and local noise. We address this limitation by formulating data selection as a coarse-to-fine hierarchical coverage problem and propose MASS. MASS learns low-dimensional principal manifold coordinates with a dense autoencoder for coarse semantic grouping, and then performs quality-aware sparse feature coverage within each group using a TopK sparse autoencoder. Experiments on Vision Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that MASS consistently outperforms strong data selection baselines across multiple budgets, and in several settings matches or surpasses full data training with only a small subset of data.

</details>


### [209] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出在严格控制标签泄漏条件下，基于WikiLeaks PlusD外交电文构建的16,000篇文档敏感性分类基准（Strategic 16K），系统评估六种模型架构（经典机器学习与Transformer），结果表明BERT在干净基准上表现最佳（Accuracy=89.14%, F1=89.33%），并提供了首个完全可复现的泄漏控制基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织文档的自动敏感性分类是一个关键但服务不足的问题，误分类可能导致监管违规或安全漏洞。AI方法虽可扩展，但其可靠性取决于训练数据的完整性。该领域普遍但未充分报告的问题是标签泄漏——文档主体中残留的分类标记使模型利用表面捷径而非学习真正基于内容的敏感性信号，导致性能估计虚高且不可靠。

Method: 作者构建了Strategic 16K语料库，包含16,000份来自WikiLeaks PlusD的外交电文，并制定了扩展的泄漏移除协议，识别并消除了嵌入文档主体中的三类残留分类标记。在此基础上，系统基准测试了六种模型架构，涵盖经典机器学习方法和基于Transformer的方法。

Result: 在干净基准上，BERT表现最强（Accuracy=89.14%, F1=89.33%），ELECTRA次之（Accuracy=88.57%, F1=88.90%）。经典模型中，TF-IDF结合逻辑回归在显著更低计算成本下达到最强性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首个完全可复现、在明确泄漏控制条件下从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的敏感性分类基准，填补了该领域缺乏泄漏控制评估的空白，为后续研究提供了可信的基准参考。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [210] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mr.Dec模型，将住院期间每天的多模态临床事件（EHR更新和胸部X光片发现）按时间顺序建模，使用Transformer解码器预测30天再入院风险，并辅以疾病特异性监督对比学习增强鲁棒性，在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将复杂的纵向住院历史压缩为固定表示，丢失了能反映患者生理状态演变的逐日级临床信号，无法捕捉住院期间临床风险随时间累积的动态轨迹。

Method: 将每次入院建模为每日多模态事件的自然时间序列（时间对齐流），整合每日EHR更新和间歇性CXR发现；利用Transformer Decoder进行序列建模；采用疾病特异性监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在潜在空间中诱导诊断感知结构。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上取得SOTA性能；模型能够识别入院期间的"关键日"，为实时风险分层提供可操作的临床解释。

Conclusion: 通过保留临床序列的完整性并建模每日多模态动态信号，该模型在再入院预测上优于现有方法，同时提供了具有临床依据的时序可解释性。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [211] [EMAN: Optimization-Driven Capacity Growth through Path Emergence in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16930)
*Chenlei Fang,Jingchen Li,Hongzong LI,Qingyao Li,Yixuan Zhang,Huarui Wu,Haobin Shi,Chunjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EMAN提出了一种优化驱动的模块化原子网络，通过潜在相对相位暴露反对称增长方向，并根据持久优化证据动态生长独立路径，在受控秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务学习方法（硬共享、多路径/专家、自适应共享、动态扩展）的能力变化受限于预定义结构或由任务边界/冲突信号触发，缺乏从单一路径出发、仅在出现持久优化证据时才生长新独立路径的机制。作者希望回答一个基本问题：网络能否从精确单路径计算起步，仅当出现持续优化证据时再生长独立的第二条路径。

Method: EMAN包含两个核心机制：其一，通过潜在相对相位（latent relative phases）暴露反对称增长方向，无需实例化第二条路径即可预判其有效性；其二，在训练过程中监测多种决策信号，将局部优化证据转化为结构性决策，仅在经过认证（certification）后才实体化两条等容量的独立路径，并自适应地分配共享与任务特定表示容量。

Result: 在受控秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2等数据集上的大量实验表明，EMAN在保持竞争性计算成本的同时实现了改进的性能。

Conclusion: EMAN证明了一个优化驱动的动态结构增长框架的有效性：网络可以从单路径开始，仅依据持续优化证据而非预定义结构或任务边界信号来生长独立路径，从而更灵活地适应多任务需求。

Abstract: Existing multi-task learning methods rely on hard sharing, multiple paths or experts, adaptive sharing, and dynamic expansion. However, their capacity changes are usually constrained by predefined structures or triggered by task boundaries and conflict signals. This raises a fundamental question: can a network start from exact single-path computation and grow a new independent path only when persistent optimization evidence appears? We propose the Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN), an optimization-driven framework for exposing an antisymmetric growth direction through latent relative phases without instantiating a second path, and for monitoring multiple decision signals during training to transform local optimization evidence into a structural decision. EMAN materializes two equal-capacity independent paths only after certification. EMAN adaptively allocates shared and task-specific representation capacity to accommodate varying task requirements. Extensive experiments on controlled rank settings, PASCAL-Context, and NYUv2 validate its effectiveness, achieving improved performance at a competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [212] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性研究了EEG基础模型在"公开编码器-私有下游模型"部署场景下的对抗迁移攻击安全问题，提出了Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE)攻击框架，通过收缩白化类原型恢复任务级决策几何，无需训练替代分类器即可生成可迁移的对抗样本，并在三个EEG任务和四种编码器上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型编码器被公开释放虽然促进了下游应用开发，但也引入了新的安全风险：公开的表征可能使私有下游模型变得脆弱。现有研究尚未探索这种"公开编码器-私有下游"架构下的对抗迁移攻击威胁，而基础模型的强迁移性可能同时意味着对抗样本的强可迁移性。

Method: 提出SW-ProxyCE攻击框架，包含三个关键要素：(1) 利用一小部分任务匹配的标记参考集；(2) 通过缩小白化（shrinkage-whitening）类原型来恢复任务级决策几何结构；(3) 实现无需查询、无需训练替代分类器的任务感知对抗样本生成，可直接在白盒公开编码器上构造可迁移到不可见下游模型的对抗扰动。

Result: 在三个EEG任务上使用三种通用基础编码器和一个范疇特定预训练编码器进行验证，覆盖线性探测和全微调两种下游模型、跨被试和被试内两种场景。结果表明：从公开编码器和有限标记参考生成的对抗样本能有效迁移到不可访问的下游模型；SW-ProxyCE在各项指标上一致优于任务无关的表征偏移攻击方法。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型的强表征迁移性并不必然带来对抗鲁棒性，公开编码器的释放需要防范对抗迁移攻击的安全风险。SW-ProxyCE证明了该威胁是现实可行的，为EEG基础模型的安全部署敲响了警钟。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable. This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [213] [DOW-KE: Anchor-Free Multi-Layer Knowledge Editing via Direct End-to-End Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16932)
*Ran Chen,Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DOW-KE是一种无锚点（anchor-free）的知识编辑方法，核心原则是"优化什么就必须部署什么"。它通过完整模型反向传播最终编辑目标，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，使跨层传播与耦合进入每个梯度步，同时将保真投影嵌入更新参数化的计算图中，确保对已部署更新施加梯度约束。在六个模型-数据集组合中的五个上取得最高总体Score和邻域Specificity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层"定位后编辑"（locate-then-edit）方法存在闭合差距（closure gap）：它们优化锚点（中间表示）但部署多层权重更新，而权重更新的联合效应从未在真实前向传播中被端到端优化。每次更新都来自局部求解，传播引起的衰减和失真无法被校正，导致锚点目标与实际编辑效果不一致。

Method: DOW-KE采用无锚点设计，将最终编辑目标通过完整模型反向传播，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，使跨层传播与层间耦合在每个梯度步中都被考虑。保真性方面，将保真投影（preservation projection）嵌入到更新参数化中并在计算图中实现，使每个梯度都作用于已部署的更新，避免后验约束重新引入闭合差距。

Result: 在大规模顺序编辑任务中，基于两个数据集和三个模型，DOW-KE在六个模型-数据集设置中的五个中取得了最高的总体Score和邻域Specificity，优于所有评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: DOW-KE验证了"优化即部署"原则的有效性：通过端到端联合优化多层权重更新并内嵌保真约束，能够消除定位-编辑方法的闭合差距，在保持编辑精准性的同时保护邻域知识，显著提升知识编辑的整体性能。

Abstract: Multi-layer locate-then-edit methods for knowledge editing first optimize target residual-stream activations (anchors) at selected layers, then realize them layer by layer as weight updates. This pipeline optimizes an intermediate representation but deploys multi-layer weight updates whose joint effect through the true forward pass is never itself optimized: regardless of how anchors are set or propagated, each update comes from a local solve, so propagation-induced attenuation and distortion go uncorrected, leaving a closure gap between anchor targets and realized edits. We propose DOW-KE, an anchor-free method built on a single principle: what is optimized must be exactly what is deployed. DOW-KE backpropagates the final editing objective through the complete model, jointly optimizing the updates of all edited layers so cross-layer propagation and coupling enter every gradient step. The same principle dictates where preservation resides: embedding the preservation projection in the update parameterization, inside the computation graph, makes every gradient act on the deployed update; post-hoc constraints would reopen the gap, and the constrained search keeps edits clear of protected knowledge. In large-scale sequential editing on two datasets and three models, DOW-KE achieves the highest overall Score and neighborhood Specificity in five of six model-dataset settings among the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [214] [Study-Strategy Clusters from EdNet Logs Track Engagement, Not Mastery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16963)
*Qingchuan Lyu,Yingxin Li,Albert Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究以EdNet-KT3数据集中5000名活跃学习者的日志为对象，检验"无监督聚类得到的ITS学习者类型应能预测学习"这一假设。通过分半时间线设计（早期聚类预测后期结果），研究发现行为聚类能预测后期参与度（坚持性η²≈0.106），但无法预测后期无辅助准确率（p_adj≈0.093）；知识追踪模型（SAKT）仅比基线小幅提升AUC（+0.051），且掌握信号与行为风格近乎独立（ARI=0.007）。结论：行为聚类描述的是学习风格与参与度，而非知识增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析领域普遍假设无监督聚类得到的ITS日志学习者类型应能预测学习结果。作者旨在系统检验这一假设是否成立，澄清行为聚类在预测学习方面的实际价值。

Method: 对EdNet-KT3中5000名活跃学习者的学习策略特征（资源使用、复习、视频、习题练习）进行聚类：首先通过轮廓系数选择父聚类（k=5，含4个对比极和约64.9%的近均值残差），再对残差重新聚类得到4个更细风格，形成8种命名策略的bootstrap稳定层次。采用分半设计：按响应数分割时间线，聚类仅用前半段，结果仅用后半段。使用SAKT知识追踪模型预测下一次正确率，并用ARI和η²评估关联。

Result: 早期聚类能预测后期参与度（坚持练习和完成后期会话，坚持性η²≈0.106，完成度η²≈0.021），但不能预测后期无辅助准确率（p_adj≈0.093，未达显著）。学习量随某些风格上升，但纯学习量聚类与策略标签匹配度极低（ARI=0.064）。SAKT在七个TOEIC考试部分上仅比基线AUC提升+0.051（CI [+0.045,+0.058]），且知识掌握信号与行为风格几乎无关（ARI=0.007）。

Conclusion: 行为聚类能够可靠地刻画学习者的学习风格和参与度模式，但其预测力局限于行为层面的持续性，无法转化为知识掌握或学习收益的预测。该研究质疑了学习分析中行为聚类等同于学习类型并可预测学习效果的前提假设，建议将行为聚类定位为参与度分析工具而非学习成就预测器。

Abstract: Learning analytics often treats unsupervised clusters of intelligent tutoring system (ITS) logs as learner types that should predict learning. We test that assumption on EdNet-KT3. Clustering study-strategy features (resource use, revision, video, problem practice) for 5{,}000 active learners yields a silhouette-selected parent cut ($k=5$) with 4 contrast poles (reading-focused, video-heavy, revision-heavy, and problem-first) plus a large near-mean residual ($\sim$64.9\%). Reclustering that residual adds four finer styles, giving a bootstrap-stable hierarchy of 8 named strategies. We split each learner's timeline by respond count so clusters use only the early half and outcomes only the late half. Early clusters predict later engagement (continuing to practice and finishing late sessions, especially persistence, $η^{2}\approx 0.106$; completion $η^{2}\approx 0.021$) but not later unassisted accuracy (correctness on late first-attempts without help; $p_{\mathrm{adj}}\approx 0.093$). Volume rises with some styles, yet volume-only clustering barely matches strategy labels (ARI$=0.064$). A knowledge-tracing model (SAKT) on the seven TOEIC exam sections predicts next correctness only modestly better than a baseline that knows only how hard each section usually is (AUC lift $+0.051$; CI $[+0.045,+0.058]$), and that mastery signal is nearly independent of behavior styles (ARI$=0.007$). Behavioral clustering here describes study styles and engagement, not knowledge gains.

</details>


### [215] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoBell-RVFL，一种基于广义bell隶属函数的质量感知随机向量功能链接网络，通过双策略样本级加权机制同时处理类别不平衡和标签噪声问题，在不牺牲RVFL闭式学习效率的前提下实现对少数类样本的严格保护和对多数类噪声/离群样本的自适应抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理类别不平衡的方法（如SMOTE和基于类权重的损失函数）主要关注类别的样本比例，却忽略了类内分布信息，导致对标签噪声和离群点鲁棒性差。随机神经网络在真实不平衡数据集上存在决策边界偏斜、忽视少数类关键样本的问题。

Method: RoBell-RVFL采用双策略样本级加权机制：(1)对少数类样本使用单位权重严格保留其信息；(2)对多数类样本通过核诱导特征空间中的概率加权广义bell（gbell）隶属函数自适应调节其影响。该方法将局部类概率和类分布信息显式融入学习过程，在保持RVFL闭式求解优势的同时实现样本贡献的自适应控制。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的广泛评估以及高达40%标签噪声的鲁棒性测试表明，RoBell-RVFL在性能上持续且显著优于最新的RVFL变体方法，验证了自适应质量感知样本加权对鲁棒RVFL学习的关键作用。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权对于实现鲁棒的RVFL学习至关重要，传统的全局加权方案（如类权重法）在噪声和不平衡环境下效果不佳；RoBell-RVFL通过局部类概率感知的样本级调节机制有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [216] [MultiSigBERT: Beyond Survival Analysis through Multimodal and Sequential Modeling in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16972)
*Paul Minchella,Stéphane Chrétien,Guillaume Metzler,Loïc Verlingue,Rémi Vaucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MultiSigBERT，一个基于路径签名表示的多模态序贯生存分析框架，融合电子健康记录中的自由文本报告与结构化数据，在超过2500名肿瘤患者、12万份医疗报告的真实队列上实现了0.743的C-index。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医疗中的机器学习通常需要整合异构数据源以改进临床决策，但现有生存模型大多局限于单一模态，且未能充分利用患者轨迹的时序性质。

Method: 多模态序贯生存建模框架：(1) 通过BERT提取并平均上下文词嵌入，将叙事性医疗报告转换为句子嵌入；(2) 通过模态特异性PCA压缩后与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时序轨迹；(3) 使用粗路径理论中的Signature变换编码跨模态高阶时序交互（无需监督）；(4) 将Signature特征作为高维特征输入LASSO正则化的Cox模型估计个体化风险评分。

Result: 在Léon Bérard中心的真实肿瘤队列（超过120,000份医疗报告、2,500多名患者）上，模型在独立测试集上达到0.743（标准差0.029）的一致性指数（C-index）。

Conclusion: 联合建模多模态时序动态与患者级几何结构对生存预测有显著益处，验证了将路径签名与深度学习嵌入结合用于多模态生存分析的可行性。

Abstract: Machine learning has become an essential component of modern healthcare, where the integration of heterogeneous data sources offers unprecedented opportunities to improve clinical decision-making. Electronic Health Records (EHR) contain complementary information -- including narrative clinical reports, numerical measurements, and structured variables -- yet most survival models remain limited to a single modality or fail to exploit the temporal nature of patient trajectories. We propose MultiSigBERT, a unified framework for multimodal sequential survival modeling in oncology based on path signature representations. Here, narrative medical reports (free-text) are converted into sentence embeddings by extracting and averaging contextual word embeddings. These representations are then compressed via modality-specific PCA and concatenated with structured covariates to form joint temporal trajectories which are then encoded using the Signature transform, a tool from Rough Paths theory that efficiently captures higher-order temporal interactions across modalities without supervision needed. The computed Signature features are finally incorporated as high dimensional features into a LASSO-regularized Cox model to estimate individualized risk scores. The performance of our novel MultiSigBERT pipeline is illustrated on the analysis of a real-world oncology cohort from the Léon Bérard Center, comprising over 120,000 medical reports and structured records from more than 2,500 patients. The model achieves a concordance index of 0.743 (sd 0.029) on an independent test set, demonstrating the benefit of jointly modeling multimodal temporal dynamics together with patient-level geometric structure for survival prediction.

</details>


### [217] [Position: Fairness Failure in Generative Models is an Evaluation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16974)
*Mariia Vladimirova,Jean-Yves Franceschi,Thibaut Issenhuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成模型公平性评估中普遍存在可复现性与可比性不足的问题，并主张通过标准化、生成模型专属的评估协议来改进。作者提出了"公平性卡片"（Fairness Cards）这一最小化报告工件，将评估选择（提示族、反事实协议、指标、拒答处理）显式化，以提升可复现性、可比性和问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在过去十年取得了突破性进展，但其公平性缺失、强化社会不平等、伤害边缘群体等问题仍未被充分关注且难以采取行动。作者认为其根本原因在于评估问题：公平性发现难以跨论文比较，也难以指导部署决策，亟需从临时性的偏见检查转向标准化、生成模型专属的评估。

Method: 作者采用立场论文（position paper）的方法论，首先诊断当前实践中反复出现的经验性和概念性失败模式，进而提出公平性卡片（Fairness Cards）作为最小化报告工件。该卡片通过显式化评估选择（提示族、反事实协议、指标、拒答处理）确保可复现性、可比性和问责性，并辅以向评估标准范式转变的附加建议。

Result: 本文未提出经验实验结果，而是给出诊断性分析与规范性建议。核心成果是公平性卡片（Fairness Cards）这一报告机制，使评估中隐性选择显式化，从而支撑跨论文比较和部署决策。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁从临时性偏见检查转向标准化、生成模型专属的评估范式，公平性卡片是实现这一转变的起点。研究还提出了进一步的建议以推动评估标准的范式转型。

Abstract: Despite groundbreaking advancements in generative models during the last decade, concerns about their lack of fairness, reinforcing societal inequalities and harming marginalized groups, remain under-addressed and difficult to act upon. This position paper argues that fairness failures in generative models, albeit driven by multiple factors, are ultimately stemming from an evaluation problem: fairness findings are rarely comparable across papers or actionable for deployment decisions. This paper diagnoses recurring empirical and conceptual failure modes in current practice and motivates a shift from ad-hoc bias checks to standardized, generative-specific evaluation. We propose Fairness Cards as a minimal reporting artifact that makes evaluation choices explicit (prompt families, counterfactual protocols, metrics, and refusal handling) enabling reproducibility, comparability, and accountability. We conclude with additional recommendations towards a paradigm shift in evaluation standards. Our project page can be found at https://mariiavladimirova.github.io/fairness-cards .

</details>


### [218] [Agents unlock new capabilities through Switching LoRA Adapters as a Tool (SLAaaT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17034)
*Kenneth Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种让智能体在长轨迹中通过工具自主切换不同LoRA适配器的方法，以克服后训练带来的灾难性遗忘问题，在合成编码任务上将能力损失（capability tax）降低至最多18倍，并优于子代理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练虽然能解锁新能力和提升专门任务性能，但往往以其他领域的灾难性遗忘为代价。在需要组合多种能力的长时间智能体轨迹中，这种权衡成为瓶颈。作者希望拒绝这种tradeoff，让单一智能体在不同能力之间自由切换而无需牺牲任何一方。

Method: 作者给智能体提供一个工具，允许其在轨迹执行中途（mid-trace）切换不同的专门化LoRA适配器。为验证其有效性，构造了两个在逻辑上简单但需要专门化的合成编码任务，并与仅使用单一适配器的智能体及子代理（subagent）方案进行对比。

Result: 该方法使模型能够解决此前无法解决的问题；模型能够自主切换适配器，并在一个任务上发现了超越人工启发式基线的新策略；相较于仅使用单一专门化适配器的智能体，能力损失最多降低18倍；同时在任务能力和token消耗上均显著优于子代理方案。

Conclusion: 通过在轨迹中动态切换LoRA适配器，可以有效规避后训练灾难性遗忘问题，实现多能力组合任务的高效求解，且不牺牲专门化性能，是一种比子代理方法更具优势的范式。

Abstract: Post-training can unlock new capabilities and improve performance on specialized tasks, but sometimes at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in other domains. This poses a problem in long agent trajectories that compose different capabilities. We reject this tradeoff by giving an agent a tool to switch between specialized LoRA adapters mid-trace. To test its effectiveness, we compose two synthetic coding tasks that are logically simple but require specialization. We find that this allows the model to solve problems it previously could not, that the model is able to switch autonomously (and find a new strategy that beats our human heuristic baseline on one task), and that this incurs an up to an 18x reduction in capability tax compared to an agent using only one specialized adapter. Our approach also substantially outperforms spawning subagents in both task capabilities and token usage.

</details>


### [219] [J-Miner: Recovering Executable Decision Knowledge from Language-Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17063)
*Yunfan Gao,Xinyi Huang,Tao Sheng,Haorui Song,Yun Xiong,Haofen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出J-Miner方法，从微调后的大语言模型分类器中挖掘其内部决策知识，将其编码为可检查、可验证、可复用的显式可执行决策规则。实验表明规则能复现高达98.3%的分类决策，行为保真度比从输入词学习的同等紧凑规则高6.0-29.5个百分点，且决策知识可迁移至轻量级学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调后可成为高性能的分类器，但仅暴露最终标签，其通过微调获得的决策知识隐式存在于模型内部，难以检查、验证和复用。作者希望将这些内部决策知识挖掘出来，以显式可执行的形式表示，使模型决策过程可解释且知识可迁移。

Method: 提出J-Miner框架，包含两个核心步骤：(1) 通过聚合跨层和跨token位置的词汇对齐内部信号，挖掘文本级别的命名概念；(2) 利用分类器自身的预测结果，在这些概念之上学习可执行的决策规则。该过程将局部内部读取值蒸馏为显式的分类器级知识表示。

Result: 在多个分类任务上，J-Miner学习的规则复现了源分类器最多98.3%的决策，比从输入词学习到的同等紧凑规则在行为保真度上高6.0-29.5个百分点。进一步分析表明命名概念反映了与任务决策相关的内部语义证据，学习到的规则将分布式信号整合为可检查的决策结构。决策知识还可迁移至轻量级学生模型（参数仅约为源分类器的1/24），同时保留源分类器平均任务准确率的99.8%。

Conclusion: 研究表明任务特定的决策知识可以被忠实地表示为显式、可执行的形式，并且可以超越其来源分类器而被复用，为可解释AI和模型知识蒸馏提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models can be fine-tuned into specialized classifiers that perform well across diverse text tasks and make complex judgments, but they typically expose only final labels, leaving the decision knowledge acquired through fine-tuning implicit within the model. We study how to mine this internal decision knowledge from a fine-tuned classifier and encode it in an executable representation that can be inspected, validated, and reused beyond the source classifier. We introduce J-Miner, which mines text-level named concepts by aggregating vocabulary-aligned internal signals across layers and token positions, and uses the classifier's own predictions to learn executable decision rules over them. This process distills local internal readouts into an explicit classifier-level knowledge representation. Across multiple classification tasks, J-Miner rules reproduce up to 98.3\% of source-classifier decisions and achieve 6.0--29.5 percentage points higher behavioral fidelity than equally compact rules learned from input words. Further analysis shows that the named concepts reflect internal semantic evidence associated with task decisions, while the learned rules consolidate these distributed signals into inspectable decision structures. The resulting decision knowledge also transfers to lightweight standalone students: using about 1/24 as many parameters as the source classifiers, they reconstruct and execute the representation from raw text while retaining 99.8\% of the source classifiers' mean task accuracy. These findings show that task-specific decision knowledge can be faithfully represented in an explicit, executable form and reused beyond the classifier in which it was learned.

</details>


### [220] [Certified but Private: Scalable Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Neural Network Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17070)
*Youwei Zhong,Ben Merbaum,Timos Antonopoulos,Ning Luo,Charalampos Papamanthou,Katerina Sotiraki,Ruzica Piskac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PANDA是一个基于零知识证明（ZKP）的可扩展系统，能够在不泄露模型私有参数的前提下，形式化证明神经网络的鲁棒性和公平性属性。其核心创新是为非线性激活层设计了一种线性松弛界的证明算法，生成轻量级证明，支持超过290万参数的神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习模型在安全关键和法律合规场景中的广泛部署，模型参数的商业机密性与对模型鲁棒性和公平性进行形式化保证的需求之间存在根本性矛盾——审计者或终端用户无法获取模型参数来进行验证。

Method: PANDA构建于CROWN鲁棒性认证框架之上，核心创新是提出了一种新颖的算法，用于证明非线性激活层的线性松弛界（linear relaxation bounds），从而生成简单轻量的零知识证明。该算法使证明复杂度与网络神经元数量呈多项式关系，而非先前系统的指数复杂度。

Result: PANDA能够在5分钟内为超过290万参数的神经网络生成本地鲁棒性的证明，并在10秒内完成验证。相比先前的基于ZKP的鲁棒性系统，PANDA支持的网络规模大4个数量级，且显著降低了证明者的开销。

Conclusion: PANDA通过多项式规模的可扩展ZKP方案，首次使得大规模神经网络的鲁棒性和公平性属性得以在保护模型隐私的前提下进行形式化验证，为安全关键场景中的模型审计提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of machine learning models, formal guarantees of the robustness and fairness of these models have become increasingly important in safety-critical and legal-compliance settings. However, model parameters are often commercial secrets that cannot be disclosed to auditors or end users. To this end, we present PANDA, a scalable system that uses zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) to prove the robustness and fairness properties of a model without revealing its private parameters. PANDA is built on top of CROWN, an efficient robustness certification framework that is used in many state-of-the-art formal verification tools for neural networks. The core contribution of PANDA is a novel algorithm for proving linear relaxation bounds for non-linear activation layers, yielding simple, lightweight proofs. Remarkably, our system can generate proofs of local robustness for neural networks with more than 2.9M parameters in 5 minutes, and can verify them in 10 seconds. Prior ZKP-based robustness system rely on exponential-time algorithms that cannot scale to nontrivial networks. In contrast, PANDA scales polynomially in the number of neurons in a network, allowing us to support neural networks 4 orders of magnitude larger than previous approaches with significantly reduced prover overhead.

</details>


### [221] [Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Calibration for Reliable Economic Forecast Intervals under Multiple Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17079)
*Bogdan Oancea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态机制感知共形预测（DRACP），一种在非可交换环境下（协变量偏移、概念漂移、局部异质性、潜在机制）实现可靠校准的分布自由预测区间方法。DRACP在统一的加权共形校准框架下结合密度比、局部核函数和概率机制感知加权，以及自调节在线显著性控制器。在48个真实经济序列上的实验中，DRACP不追求最高效率（强自适应在线共形预测区间约窄20%），但提供最可靠的校准：覆盖率最接近标称0.90（实际0.890），任何序列上不低于0.80，在所有预测时域上保持最佳覆盖率，并在2021-2023年通胀激增期间表现最佳。消融研究表明在线控制器和条件尺度归一化贡献最大，加权成分贡献较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形预测依赖于可交换性假设，但经济预测中该假设常被违反（协变量偏移、概念漂移、局部异质性和潜在机制）。现有加权共形方法缺乏对非平稳经济环境的稳健适配，需要一种统一的框架来同时处理这些问题并在在线场景下保证覆盖率。

Method: DRACP将密度比加权、局部核加权和概率机制感知加权与自调节在线显著性控制器统一到加权共形校准框架中。理论层面给出三个结果：在理想重要性权重下有限样本有效性、估计权重的覆盖率缺口边界（以有效样本量为率）、在线控制器的确定性或遗憾保证。实验使用48个真实预测序列（欧元区和EU-27 HICP通胀、美国宏观与能源指标、日度金融序列），与六种基线对比，其中FACI、强自适应在线共形预测和共形PID使用作者原实现验证。

Result: DRACP在覆盖率可靠性上最优：实测覆盖率0.890最接近标称0.90，所有序列上不低于0.80，各预测时域上保持最佳覆盖率，并在2021-2023通胀期表现最好。强自适应方法在48个序列中的20个欠覆盖（DRACP仅10个）。但效率上DRACP不如强自适应方法（后者区间约窄20%）。消融实验表明在线控制器和条件尺度归一化是主要性能来源，加权成分贡献较小。

Conclusion: DRACP为经济预测提供了一个在覆盖率可靠性与区间效率之间具有原则性权衡的方案。当预测区间必须满足覆盖率标准时，DRACP是更优选择；若追求区间效率，强自适应在线共形预测更合适。该工作为鲁棒共形预测在非平稳经济环境中的实际应用提供了系统性的实证与理论支撑。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free prediction intervals but relies on exchangeability, an assumption often violated in economic forecasting because of covariate shift, concept drift, local heterogeneity and latent regimes. We propose Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Prediction (DRACP), which combines density-ratio, localized kernel and probabilistic regime-aware weighting with a self-tuning online significance controller in a unified weighted conformal calibration framework. We distinguish three theoretical results: finite-sample validity under oracle importance weights, a coverage-gap bound for estimated weights with rates in effective sample size, and deterministic or regret guarantees for the online controller. We evaluate DRACP against six baselines on 48 real forecasting series covering euro-area and EU-27 HICP inflation, US macroeconomic and energy indicators, and daily financial series. Recent online methods (FACI, strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction and conformal PID) were verified against the authors' implementations. DRACP is not the most efficient method: strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction achieves the best interval score and intervals about 20% narrower. Instead, DRACP provides the most reliable calibration, achieving coverage closest to the nominal 0.90 (0.890), never falling below 0.80 on any series, maintaining the best coverage at all forecast horizons, and performing best during the 2021-2023 inflation surge. The strongly-adaptive method undercovers on 20 of 48 series versus 10 for DRACP. DRACP therefore offers a principled trade-off between calibration and efficiency, favoring reliable coverage when prediction intervals must satisfy coverage standards. An ablation study shows that the online controller and conditional-scale normalization provide most of the performance gain, whereas the weighting components make a smaller contribution.

</details>


### [222] [Backward through Time, Algebraically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17087)
*Konstantinos Kogkalidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个代数泛型且可微分的线性时序逻辑（LTL）评估引擎telos，它以PyTorch库的形式提供，允许用户在不同语义代数之间切换以处理软值系统（如神经策略、自适应控制器等）中的时序属性评估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL的传统布尔语义无法为软值系统（神经网络策略、自适应控制器、序列模型等）提供有效的训练信号。虽然已存在各种可微分语义，但现有实现多为浅嵌入，要求用户在使用前就锁定一种语义代数及其隐式行为，缺乏灵活性和可移植性。

Method: 论文将读者置于函数式程序员的视角，拒绝接受"必须事先承诺单一语义代数"的困境，由此设计了一个代数泛型（algebra-generic）的可微分评估引擎，并提供了一个可执行规范来界定引擎能接受的各类代数。作者实现了多种代数，并从前向和反向传播两个方向审计它们的行为。

Result: 作者实现了多种语义代数并对它们的前向与反向传播行为进行了审计。关键洞察是：每种代数本质上都是"选择在哪个方向上令人失望（disappoint）以及如何失望"——即不同代数在保守/乐观、上近似/下近似等维度上有系统性差异。全部实现已打包为PyTorch库telos并开源。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种统一的、代数泛型的可微LTL评估框架，使用户无需预先绑定单一语义即可在软值系统中灵活运用时序逻辑约束作为训练信号，并通过开源库telos交付了可复现的实现。

Abstract: Linear temporal logic is a modal extension of propositional logic that allows one to state how a system should behave over time. Its canonical domain is the booleans, but discretely-valued judgements are of little use in steering softly-valued systems (neural policies, adaptive controllers, sequence models, etc). In such cases, the goal formula's (dis)satisfaction becomes a training signal, and differentiability becomes a prime concern. Candidate differentiable semantics abound, but navigating them is tricky. Implementations, where available, are shallow embeddings, demanding an upfront commitment to a single semantic algebra and its (usually implicit) conduct.
