The paper casts the reader as a functional programmer asked to come to terms with this predicament, and refusing. Out of that refusal comes an evaluation engine that is algebra-generic and amenable to differentiation, together with an executable specification of the algebras it can accept. Various algebras are implemented and audited for their behavior, both forward and backward. Each algebra turns out to be a choice of which direction to disappoint, and how. Everything described (and more) is part of the PyTorch library telos, to be found at https://github.com/konstantinosKokos/telos.

</details>


### [223] [Deep Learning for Cross-Border Electricity Price Forecasting: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17091)
*Hadeer Elashhab,Sai Srijan Papineni,Marvin Dorn,Veit Hagenmeyer,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用公开电力市场数据，建立了一个可复现的电力价格预测（EPF）基准评估框架，对六种深度学习模型（涵盖状态空间、MLP、RNN和Transformer架构）在跨市场场景下进行系统比较，并在德国-卢森堡（DE-LU）2024年竞价区的低数据目标市场条件下（零样本、单样本和少样本学习）进行测试。研究发现N-HiTS和NBEATSx在有限数据场景下表现优异，Transformer模型虽可达到可比精度但需要更多调参适配，最强模型间差异通常较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场数据公开可用但对预测研究而言缺乏公认的基准数据集，导致各研究采用不同数据集和指标在孤立环境中评估方法，难以一致性地评估进展和比较最先进方法，因此需要建立可复现的标准化评估框架。

Method: 使用标准化数据集（包含日历特征、历史价格和市场衍生特征），对六种深度学习模型（覆盖状态空间、MLP、RNN和Transformer架构）进行跨市场泛化能力的比较评估；通过零样本、单样本和少样本学习模拟低数据目标市场条件；测试集聚焦2024年德国-卢森堡（DE-LU）竞价区。

Result: N-HiTS和NBEATSx在有限数据场景下表现具有竞争力；Transformer模型可达到可比精度但需要更多适配和调参；模型性能受益于精细的特征选择和超参数调优；最强模型之间的差异通常很小。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可复现的EPF基准评估框架，指出在有限数据条件下，结构化较强的模型（如N-HiTS和NBEATSx）更具优势，而Transformer需要更多调参成本；不同强模型间的性能差距有限，特征工程和超参数优化对性能提升同样重要。

Abstract: While publicly available electricity market data presents a valuable resource for forecasting research, the field lacks established benchmark datasets for standardized comparison. As a result, many studies have relied on different datasets and metrics to evaluate methods in isolated settings, making it difficult to assess progress and compare state-of-the-art approaches consistently. In this work, we use public data to evaluate deep learning models for electricity price forecasting (EPF) across multiple market settings. Our goal is to establish a reproducible framework that enables a consistent evaluation of forecasting models. Although deep learning has been explored for day-ahead EPF, many prior studies are limited to single-market settings, narrow feature sets, or fixed training regimes. This work presents a comparative evaluation of six deep learning models--covering state-space, MLP, RNN, and Transformer-based architectures--emphasizing generalization across markets. We simulate low-data target-market conditions using zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot learning. Our test set focuses on the Germany-Luxembourg (DE-LU) bidding zone in 2024 using a standardized dataset with calendar, historical price, and market-derived features. Our findings suggest that N-HiTS and NBEATSx perform competitively in limited-data scenarios, while transformer-based models can reach comparable accuracy but tend to require more adaptation and tuning. Model performance also benefits from careful feature selection and hyperparameter tuning, and we note that the differences between the strongest models are often small.

</details>


### [224] [From Abductive Explanations to Global Logical Rules for Node Classification in SGCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17103)
*Bryan Lima Cavalcante,Thiago Alves Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于极小溯因解释（minimal abductive explanations）的逻辑框架，用于解释SGC网络在节点分类任务中的预测。通过计算每个节点的最小特征-节点对集合来训练决策树，进而提取全局逻辑规则，在保持高保真度的同时生成紧凑的全局规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑解释方法（如LogicXGNN）从解释子图中提取全局逻辑规则，但这些子图可能包含冗余的结构信息，且局限于单个节点，限制了所提取规则的泛化性。

Method: 提出一种基于逻辑的框架，用于SGC网络的节点分类。具体步骤：(1) 为每个节点计算一组最小节点-特征对，足以保持预测类别不变；(2) 利用这些极小溯因解释作为中间表示来训练决策树；(3) 从决策树中提取全局逻辑规则。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架能够生成紧凑的全局规则，同时保持对原始SGC模型的高保真度。

Conclusion: 通过使用极小溯因解释作为中间表示，可以有效减少解释中的冗余信息，生成更具泛化性和紧凑性的全局逻辑规则，同时确保解释的保真度。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable performance in node classification tasks, motivating growing interest in methods capable of explaining their predictions. Recent logic-based approaches, such as LogicXGNN, derive global logical rules for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) from collections of explanatory subgraphs. While informative, these subgraphs may contain redundant structural information that is specific to individual nodes, potentially limiting the generality of the extracted rules. In this work, we propose a logic-based framework for node classification in Simple Graph Convolution (SGC) networks that uses minimal abductive explanations as an intermediate representation for rule extraction. For each node, we compute a minimal set of node-feature pairs sufficient to preserve the predicted class. These explanations are then used to train decision trees from which global logical rules are extracted. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact global rules while maintaining high fidelity to the original SGC model.

</details>


### [225] [Causal Discovery in Equal Variance Linear Gaussian DAGs via SURE-Tuned Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17132)
*Sambit Mishra,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SURE-Ridge，一种非迭代、闭式解的线性高斯结构方程模型DAG估计器。该方法通过并行节点回归和SURE自适应选择正则化参数，并采用自适应阈值化提取DAG，在样本有限和计算有限两种场景下均优于NOTEARS、DAGMA和GBNSL基线，实现了最低的结构汉明距离和最短运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续优化方法（如梯度下降）需要逐问题超参数调优，且迭代过程在样本有限（样本数接近或小于节点数）和计算有限两种实际重要场景下表现不佳，因此需要一种非迭代且无需繁琐调参的解决方案。

Method: SURE-Ridge采用闭式解的非迭代方法：对每个节点并行执行岭回归（Ridge Regression），正则化参数通过Stein无偏风险估计（SURE）自适应选择，随后应用自适应阈值化过程从软邻接矩阵中提取DAG。

Result: 在数值实验中，SURE-Ridge在小样本场景下取得最低的结构汉明距离（SHD），在所有测试样本量下均取得最短运行时间，全面优于NOTEARS、DAGMA和GBNSL三个基线方法。

Conclusion: SURE-Ridge通过闭式解和非迭代设计，有效解决了样本有限和计算有限场景下因果发现方法的瓶颈，在准确性和效率两方面同时实现最优表现，为等方差线性高斯SEM的DAG恢复提供了实用且高效的方案。

Abstract: Recovering the directed acyclic graph (DAG) of a structural equation model (SEM) from observational data is a central problem in causal discovery. The iterative gradient descent and per-problem hyperparameter tuning of continuous-optimization methods are poorly suited to two practically important regimes: the sample-limited regime, where the number of samples is comparable to or smaller than the number of nodes in the DAG, and the compute-limited regime. This work proposes SURE-Ridge, a non-iterative, closed-form estimator for equal variance linear Gaussian SEM. The method performs parallel node-wise regressions with regularization parameters chosen adaptively by Stein's unbiased risk estimate (SURE), and applies an adaptive thresholding procedure to extract a DAG from the resulting soft adjacency matrix. Numerical results show that SURE-Ridge achieves the lowest structural Hamming distance in the small-sample regime and the lowest run time across all sample sizes tested, compared with NOTEARS, DAGMA, and GBNSL baselines.

</details>


### [226] [Iterative tensor network transformations for element-wise evaluation of elementary and filtering functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17135)
*Xiao Wang,Tomohiro Hashizume,Pia Siegl,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了迭代张量网络变换（ITNTs），一种在张量列（TT）压缩域中逐元素计算非线性函数和滤波函数的通用算法框架，使张量网络方法能够高效处理大规模数据和优化问题，并在3D反应流场计算和高达2^70配置空间的Max-SAT求解中展示了其能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络是压缩大规模数据的强大格式，但其在通用数据处理中的应用受到非线性操作难以执行的限制。

Method: 引入迭代张量网络变换（ITNTs），一种通用算法框架，用于在张量列（TT）编码的数据上逐元素评估初等和非线性滤波函数。该方法完全在压缩域中运行，实现对指数级大小数据集的高效计算，同时保持受控的计算成本。

Result: 在两个关键领域展示了优越性：（I）在3D反应流场上评估高度非线性的初等和滤波函数，实现高保真反应速率计算和区域滤波；（II）在复杂的优化问题中发现极值，例如求解高达2^70配置空间上的Max-SAT实例。

Conclusion: ITNT被确立为一种基础性工具，赋予张量网络方法通用型数据科学和大规模优化的能力。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful formats for compressing large-scale data. However, their application to general data processing has been limited by the difficulty of performing nonlinear operations. Here, we introduce iterative tensor network transformations (ITNTs), a general algorithmic framework for the element-wise evaluation of elementary and nonlinear filtering functions on data encoded as tensor trains (TTs), a class of tensor networks. Our approach operates entirely in the compressed domain, enabling efficient computation on exponentially large datasets while maintaining a controlled computational cost. We demonstrate its power in two key areas: (I) evaluating highly nonlinear elementary and filtering functions on a 3D reactive flow field, enabling high-fidelity reaction rate computation and region filtering, and (II) finding extrema in complex optimization problems, such as solving Max-SAT instances on spaces up to $2^{70}$ configurations. These results establish ITNT as a foundational tool that provides tensor network methods with the capability for general-purpose data science and large-scale optimization.

</details>


### [227] [OraclePhys: A Systematic Framework for LLM Fine-Tuning on Structural Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17162)
*Mingyu Li,Guorui Song,Jing Lin,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OraclePhys系统化微调框架，通过实验证明：标签的答案形式（而非比特数）因果性地决定微调学到什么——排序目标能安装分布外前向模型，标量目标仅部分有效，布尔目标无可检测效果；而GRPO式的优势加权评分仅提升奖励，却使模型在held-out物理任务上与初始状态统计等价。训练出的8B模型成为首个在空间结构响应任务上达到数据精度前沿的LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常在事后诊断语言模型从微调中内化了什么，缺乏将标签结构作为可控实验变量的系统性研究。作者希望回答"微调过程中标签的哪些属性因果性地决定了模型学到什么"，从而建立可预测的微调理论。

Method: 构建OraclePhys框架，包含三部分：(1)OraclePhys-Bench，一个精确评分的结构力学基准，用有限元oracle对每个答案和反事实编辑评分，无需人工标注或LLM裁判；(2)OraclePhys-30K，包含七种答案形式的监督数据集，使用字节级相同的结构描述；(3)跨七种答案形式和三种验证者角色的受控训练研究。通过多域、多模型族、改写评估面进行稳健性验证。

Result: 两大发现：(1)标签的答案形式因果决定微调效果——排序目标安装分布外前向模型（未训练基础模型处于猜测先验），标量目标至多部分安装，布尔目标无可检测效果；向量-标量鸿沟在第二个物理域、第二个模型族和改写评估面上均稳健。(2)书面或分数过滤的答案安装能力，而GRPO优势加权评分提升奖励但使模型在held-out物理上统计等价于起点，仅够用于路由。训练的8B达到任务数据精度前沿，超过前沿LLM零样本与32样本表现，达到专家水平。

Conclusion: 研究表明标签对目标计算的"详细说明程度"决定微调教会什么——标签形式是关键因素，使用什么训练决定路由选择。这为预测性微调提供了实证基础，指出单纯优化奖励（如GRPO）并不等同于内化物理能力。

Abstract: What a language model internalizes from fine-tuning is usually diagnosed after the fact. We make it an experimental variable. OraclePhys is a systematic fine-tuning framework with three components: OraclePhys-Bench, an exactly-graded structural-mechanics benchmark whose finite-element oracle scores every answer and counterfactual edit -- no human labels, no LLM judging; OraclePhys-30K, a supervision dataset of seven answer forms over byte-identical structure descriptions; and a controlled training study across the seven forms and three verifier roles. The study yields two findings. First, the label's answer form -- not its bit count -- causally determines what fine-tuning teaches: a ranking objective installs an out-of-distribution forward model where the untrained base sits at the guessing prior, a scalar objective at best a partial one, a boolean nothing detectable; the vector-scalar gulf survives a second physics domain, a second model family, and a paraphrased evaluation surface. Second, written or score-filtered answers install this capability, while advantage-weighted scores (GRPO) raise reward yet leave the model statistically equivalent to its start on held-out physics -- within the recipes and budgets tested -- sufficing only for routing. The trained 8B -- the first LLM on spatial structural response -- reaches the task's data-precision frontier: above a frontier LLM at zero- and 32-shot, at a specialist's level. What the label spells out about the target computation is what fine-tuning teaches; what you train on is what you route.

</details>


### [228] [Q-Learning With World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17163)
*Perry Dong,Yueru Jia,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QWM是一个在标准Q-learning之上利用世界模型进行测试时搜索的框架：策略和价值函数仅在真实转移上训练，搜索过程在想象轨迹上进行以选择高价值动作，从而避免模型复合偏差同时获得预测搜索的样本效率优势，在Robomimic和LIBERO基准上显著超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略强化学习在样本效率上已有显著提升，但世界模型在RL中的应用主要局限于监督式策略学习或直接在想象轨迹上优化策略/价值函数，后者容易出现复合偏差（compounding bias），且难以扩展到真实机器人等大规模高维问题上，问题随任务时域和视觉复杂度而恶化。作者希望回答：能否直接在标准Q-learning之上利用世界模型来提升性能，同时保持训练基于真实的在线设定？

Method: 提出QWM框架：在世界模型生成的想象轨迹上进行测试时搜索（test-time search），叠加在Q-learning之上，在在线rollout和评估阶段选择高价值动作。关键设计是策略和价值函数仅在真实转移（real transitions）上训练，从而避免复合模型偏差，同时仍能获得预测搜索带来的样本效率收益。

Result: 在具有挑战性的操作基准Robomimic和LIBERO上，QWM在样本效率和最终性能两方面均显著优于现有的强基线方法（prior state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: QWM证明了将世界模型与标准Q-learning结合、仅在真实数据上训练策略/价值函数、同时在测试时利用想象轨迹进行搜索是一种有效的范式，既能避免模型复合偏差，又能提升样本效率，在机器人操作任务上展现出优异的可扩展性和性能。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) has become increasingly sample-efficient, enabling applications such as RL fine-tuning of Vision-Language-Action models into reliable, high-performing policies. World models offer a further lever for sample efficiency, as they predict state changes rather than actions alone, but their success has largely been confined to supervised policy learning. Prior model-based RL methods often optimize the policy or value function directly on imagined rollouts, which is prone to compounding bias and struggles to scale to large, high-dimensional problems such as real-world robotics, a problem that worsens with task horizon and visual complexity. In this work, we instead ask whether we can leverage world models directly on top of standard Q-learning to improve performance, while remaining trained and grounded in the real, online setting. We propose QWM, a framework that leverages world models to perform test-time search over imagined trajectories on top of Q-learning to select high-value actions during both online rollouts and evaluation. Since the policy and value function are trained only on real transitions, QWM avoids compounding model bias while still gaining the sample-efficiency benefits of predictive search. On challenging manipulation benchmarks Robomimic and LIBERO, QWM significantly outperforms strong prior state-of-the-art methods on both sample efficiency and performance.

</details>


### [229] [SCENARIODIFF: A Scenario-level Guidance Framework for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting--Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17164)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Dat Nguyen Cong,Duc-Trong Le,Thanh Trung Huynh,Tung Kieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCENARIODIFF提出了一种层级上下文推理框架，通过将文本信息组织为历史证据、情景描述和锚点引导三个层次，来增强多模态时间序列预测，尤其适用于事件驱动的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测方法难以有效利用文本上下文，因为多模态方法要么让LLM直接预测数值导致难以解释，要么隐式融合文本与时间序列导致上下文影响不可控。特别是在外部事件驱动、历史值尚未反映未来动态的情况下，需要更显式和可解释的文本身上下文引导方式。

Method: 提出SCENARIODIFF三层级框架：(1) Historical Context Agent从原始文档中逐步提取证据；(2) Scenario Agent为预测区间生成定性情景描述；(3) Anchor Guidance Agent为事件相关未来区域生成稀疏锚点。这些结构化信号条件化一个多模态Diffusion Transformer，并通过Anchor Blended Sampling在不重新训练的情况下局部细化生成轨迹。

Result: 在Time-MMD基准上的实验表明，SCENARIODIFF在事件驱动领域特别有效，证明了显式层级情景引导对多模态时间序列预测的价值。

Conclusion: 显式的层级化情景引导（历史证据→情景描述→锚点）能够使文本上下文对时间序列预测的影响变得可解释、可控制且有效，尤其在文档存在噪声和弱对齐的多模态场景下表现突出。

Abstract: Textual context such as news, reports, and logs can provide valuable signals for time series forecasting, especially when future dynamics are driven by external events that are not yet visible in historical values. Existing multimodal forecasting methods often either ask large language models (LLMs) to predict numerical values directly or fuse text and time series implicitly, making contextual influence difficult to interpret and control. We propose SCENARIODIFF, a hierarchical contextual reasoning framework for multimodal time series forecasting under noisy and weakly aligned documents. SCENARIODIFF organizes contextual information into three levels: a Historical Context Agent extracts stepwise evidence from raw documents, a Scenario Agent produces a qualitative scenario description for the forecast horizon, and an Anchor Guidance Agent generates sparse anchor points for event-relevant future regions. These structured signals condition a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer, while Anchor Blended Sampling locally refines generated trajectories without retraining. Experiments on the Time-MMD benchmark show that SCENARIODIFF is especially effective in event-driven domains, demonstrating the value of explicit hierarchical scenario guidance for multimodal time series forecasting. Our full implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ScenarioDiff_ICDM-2C4C

</details>


### [230] [Population Health-Based Machine Learning Reveals Associations Between Psychosocial Factors and Chronic Kidney Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17174)
*Md. Atik Shams,David Eisenberg,Sumaiya Fatema,Asma Sultana,D. M Hasibul Islam,Junnatul Mawa,Anindita Datta,Nafiya Ahmed,Danastan Tasaouf Mridula,SK. Sazid Mahmud,Simon Bin Akter,Tanjila Helaly,Jorge Fresneda Fernandez,Humayera Islam,Tanmoy Sarkar Pias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合大规模远程健康数据与机器学习的两部分研究，通过定制堆叠集成模型对慢性肾脏病（CKD）状态进行分类，并利用SHAP分析识别关键疾病驱动因素。模型在四个大型数据集上实现了72.56-76.12%的平衡准确率和79.59-82.29%的AUROC，发现定期体检、年龄、血压和心理健康压力是重要预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾脏病（CKD）进展无声且严重损害生活质量，早期检测对改善患者预后至关重要。作者旨在利用大规模远程健康数据和先进的机器学习方法实现对CKD的自报状态分类，并识别疾病的关键驱动因素，从而为临床早期干预提供支持。

Method: 研究采用两阶段方法：(1) 从BRFSS 2021（438,693样本）、BRFSS 2019（418,268样本）、NHIS 2021（29,482样本）和NHIS 2020（31,568样本）四个大型健康调查数据集中选择特征；使用九种最先进的数据插补方法处理缺失值，并通过采样策略缓解类别不平衡问题；(2) 构建定制化堆叠集成模型进行分类，随后使用SHapley Additive exPlanations（SHAP）进行特征重要性分析，并经临床审查验证关键预测因子。

Result: 定制堆叠集成模型在四个数据集上取得了72.56-76.12%的平衡准确率和79.59-82.29%的AUROC分数。SHAP分析结合临床审查揭示了关键预测因子，包括定期医疗检查、年龄、血压和心理健康压力指标。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个稳健且可解释的CKD风险分层框架，以及对其相关因素的可操作性洞察，为CKD的早期检测和风险因素管理提供了有价值的方法学基础和临床参考。

Abstract: Chronic kidney disease (CKD) progresses silently and severely undermines quality of life, making early detection critical for improving patient outcomes. We present a two-part study that combines large-scale telehealth data with advanced machine learning to both classify self-reported CKD status and identify key drivers of disease. Using selected features from the Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System (BRFSS 2021: 438,693 samples; BRFSS 2019: 418,268 samples) and the National Health Interview Survey (NHIS 2021: 29,482 samples; NHIS 2020: 31,568 samples), we addressed missing data with nine state-of-the-art imputation methods and mitigated class imbalance via sampling strategies. Our customized stacked ensemble model achieved balanced accuracy of 72.56-76.12%, with corresponding AUROC scores of 79.59-82.29%. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis, followed by clinical review, highlighted critical predictors, including regular medical check-ups, age, blood pressure, and indicators of mental health stress. These findings deliver a robust and interpretable framework for CKD risk stratification and provide actionable insights into its associated factors.

</details>


### [231] [Task Specialization Fine-Tuning for Contextual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17180)
*Jianan Zhou,Jung-Hoon Cho,Tianyue Zhou,Han Zheng,Jie Zhang,Roy Dong,Yining Ma,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TSFT框架，通过预训练单一策略后进行多策略微调，利用参数模型预测微调性能并用整数线性规划求解预算分配，实现样本高效的上下文强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL方法通常从零训练，依赖多任务学习或策略训练，缺乏利用预训练策略进行微调的统一范式；同时预训练后的多策略微调面临异质边际收益和样本低效等独特挑战。

Method: TSFT在线框架：用简单参数模型预测每个任务区域的微调性能，将预算分配问题形式化为整数线性规划并精确求解，在给定预训练策略和有限预算下最优分配各任务区域的微调资源。

Result: 在组合优化、连续控制和LLM微调等多个决策领域的大量实验中，TSFT显著优于基线方法，在任务覆盖率上接近oracle性能。

Conclusion: TSFT为CRL提供了预训练-微调范式下的新方向，证明基于模型的CRL可以与现代预训练微调时代对齐，实现样本高效的策略特化。

Abstract: Contextual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) seeks to generalize classical RL by maximizing task coverage across a context space of related tasks. While prior works often train from scratch and rely on either multi-task learning for a single policy or strategically training multiple policies, we advocate for a unified alternative: pretraining a single policy with good initial performance, followed by fine-tuning multiple policies for task specialization. This new paradigm, however, introduces unique challenges, such as heterogeneous marginal returns and sample inefficiency. This raises a critical research question: given a pretrained policy and a constrained budget, how much fine-tuning should each task region receive to enable sample-efficient CRL? To this end, we propose Task Specialization Fine-Tuning (TSFT), an online framework that predicts fine-tuning performance with a simple parametric model and exactly solves the resulting discrete budget allocation problem via integer linear programming. Extensive experiments across diverse decision domains, including combinatorial optimization, continuous control, and LLM fine-tuning, demonstrate that TSFT significantly outperforms baselines in task coverage and approaches oracle performance. Our work charts a new direction for model-based CRL, aligning with the modern pretrain-finetune era.

</details>


### [232] [Reinforcement Learning as (Discrete) Potential Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17181)
*Christopher Connolly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了概率论与位势理论之间的深层联系，并从位势理论的视角审视固定策略假设下的强化学习核心表示与算法，探讨这一视角可能带来的样本效率提升和形式化约束，并指出当放松固定策略假设时，线性位势理论框架可自然扩展至非线性情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习理论从根本上依赖于马尔可夫链所涉及的概率论，而概率论与位势理论之间存在深刻联系。作者希望通过探索位势理论视角，为强化学习提供改进样本效率和形式化约束的新路径。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性工作，首先回顾概率论与位势理论的联系，然后系统性地从位势理论视角审视固定策略假设下强化学习的核心表示（如值函数、策略评估等）与算法。在此基础上，讨论放松固定策略假设后，线性位势理论框架向非线性情形的自然扩展。

Result: 揭示了位势理论为强化学习核心概念与算法提供的替代性理论视角，该视角可能带来样本效率的提升和形式化约束的引入；同时指出固定策略假设放松时，位势理论框架可向非线性推广。

Conclusion: 位势理论为强化学习提供了有价值的理论新视角，尤其在固定策略假设下表现出潜在优势；未来工作可进一步探索非线性扩展及其对强化学习算法设计与样本效率的实际影响。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) theory fundamentally depends on probability theory through the Markov chain. There is a deep connection between probability theory and potential theory. This paper reviews that connection and explores the potential-theoretic viewpoint for core reinforcement learning representations and algorithms under a fixed-policy assumption. This viewpoint may offer a path for improved sample efficiency and formal constraints that can be applied to RL. When the fixed-policy assumption is relaxed, the linear potential theory framework can be naturally extended to the nonlinear case.

</details>


### [233] [How smoothing the affinity matrix affects neighborhood preservation in t-SNE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17190)
*Shirin Mohebi,Guillaume Bied,Jefrey Lijffijt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究t-SNE中亲和矩阵概率分布的"尖锐度"（sharpness）对多尺度邻域保持的影响，提出一种行向幂变换（由参数gamma控制），可平滑或锐化亲和矩阵每一行，同时保持稀疏性和秩序。实验表明锐化改善最近邻保持，平滑改善更广局部邻域保持，在中局部范围优于包括多尺度方法在内的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE是广泛使用的降维可视化方法，其核心是亲和矩阵中对称化概率的优化问题。然而，现有研究缺乏对概率分布尖锐度如何影响不同尺度邻域保持的系统性分析。理解这一关系有助于更好地控制t-SNE的局部结构保持性能。

Method: 引入一个由参数gamma控制的行向幂变换，该变换可以在保持稀疏性和秩序的前提下平滑或锐化亲和矩阵的每一行。作者证明该变换等价于重新缩放高斯带宽（即改变困惑度），但由于概率分布尖锐度逐点变化，固定gamma会导致逐点有效困惑度不同，因此不同于全局改变困惑度。

Result: 经验实验发现：锐化（sharpening）改善最近邻（very nearest neighbors）的保持，而平滑（smoothing）改善更广泛局部邻域的保持，在中局部范围（mid-local range）优于包括多尺度方法在内的替代亲和矩阵构造方案。

Conclusion: 通过gamma控制的亲和矩阵幂变换为t-SNE提供了一种灵活的逐点尺度调节机制，在不同邻域尺度上能够优于现有方法，为降维可视化中的邻域保持提供更精细的控制手段。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction methods are instrumental to visualize high-dimensional data, and t-SNE stands as one of the most widely used methods due to its emphasis on local neighborhood preservation. A central component of t-SNE is the affinity matrix, which expresses pairwise similarities in the form of symmetrized probabilities, over which the optimization problem of t-SNE is defined. We study how the sharpness of this probability distribution affects neighborhood preservation at different scales. We introduce a row-wise power transform controlled by a parameter gamma that can smooth or sharpen each row of the affinity matrix while preserving sparsity and rank order. We show that this transform is equivalent to rescaling the Gaussian bandwidth and thus to changing the perplexity. However, as the sharpness of the probability distribution varies per point, a fixed gamma leads to point-dependent effective perplexities, making it distinct from changing the global perplexity. Empirically, we find that sharpening improves preservation of the very nearest neighbors, while smoothing improves preservation of broader local neighborhoods, outperforming alternative affinity constructions including multiscale methods in the mid-local range.

</details>


### [234] [Pessimistic Meta-Induction and Its Limits: Lessons from Frequentist Statistics and Machine Learning Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17213)
*Hanti Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过削弱悲观的元归纳论证的归纳步骤而非历史前提来挑战它对科学实在论的攻击，主张普通枚举归纳在收敛于真理方面优于元归纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悲观的元归纳论证是科学实在论面临的主要挑战之一，该论证从科学史上理论被取代的事实归纳出"当前科学理论也将被取代"的结论。已有反驳多针对其历史前提（即归纳基础），本文试图从新的角度切入，通过攻击其归纳步骤本身来削弱该论证。

Method: 借鉴频率主义统计学、机器学习和形式认识论中关于科学推断的一般认识论框架，从收敛于真理（convergence to the truth）的视角评估归纳推理。比较普通枚举归纳与元归纳在"处处收敛"（everywhere convergence）与"几乎处处收敛"（almost everywhere convergence）两种收敛标准下的表现。

Result: 普通枚举归纳能够实现"处处收敛"，而元归纳甚至无法实现"几乎处处收敛"。更进一步，在元归纳所产生的问题情境中，不存在任何一种推断方法能够实现几乎处处收敛。

Conclusion: 元归纳的归纳步骤在收敛性标准下存在根本缺陷，其失败并非偶然而是问题结构本身所决定的，从而削弱了悲观元归纳论证对科学实在论的威胁。

Abstract: This paper challenges the pessimistic meta-inductive argument against scientific realism by undermining its inductive step rather than its historical premise. Although related challenges already exist, I develop a new one. Drawing on a general epistemology of scientific inference developed in frequentist statistics, machine learning, and formal epistemology, I evaluate induction in terms of convergence to the truth. I argue that ordinary enumerative induction can achieve everywhere convergence, whereas meta-induction fails even to achieve almost everywhere convergence. Indeed, in the problem context where meta-induction arises, the failure is deeper: no inference method whatsoever achieves almost everywhere convergence.

</details>


### [235] [Delta2Gamma: Band-Wise Adaptive Contrastive Learning of EEG for Alzheimer's Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17231)
*Chanwoo Park,Chanwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Delta2Gamma，一种基于频带分解的自监督EEG表征学习框架，通过对比学习从无标注数据中学习脑电特征，在阿尔茨海默病筛查中达到92.4%的准确率，超越监督基线和现有专用EEG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 痴呆症的低成本、可扩展筛查仍是开放问题。基于影像的诊断成本高难以广泛部署，而EEG虽便携廉价但存在噪声大、跨个体差异大、临床标注少的问题，亟需一种能从无标注数据中学习的表征方法。

Method: Delta2Gamma是一个自监督框架，通过对比增强视图来学习EEG表征。核心创新在于：将每个脑电记录分解为五种经典神经节律（delta、theta、alpha、beta、gamma），每个频带配备独立的编码器和投影头，并在对比训练中自适应预测每个频带的温度参数，以自动平衡不同频带间信号统计特性的差异。

Result: 在ADFTD队列上采用严格的留一被试（leave-one-subject-out）协议，Delta2Gamma在区分阿尔茨海默病与认知正常对照时达到92.4%的准确率，超过了监督骨干网络和近期专用EEG方法。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了频带解耦与自适应温度调制的自监督对比学习能有效利用无标注EEG数据，为低成本、可扩展的痴呆症筛查提供了有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Low-cost, scalable screening for dementia remains an open problem. Imaging-based diagnosis is costly and hard to deploy widely. Electroencephalography (EEG) is portable and inexpensive, but its recordings are noisy, vary widely across subjects, and carry few clinical labels. We tackle this with Delta2Gamma, a self-supervised framework that learns EEG representations from unlabeled data by contrasting augmented views of each signal. Rather than treat EEG as a single stream, Delta2Gamma decomposes every recording into the five canonical neural rhythms (delta, theta, alpha, beta, gamma). Each band gets its own encoder and projection head. Each also gets a temperature that is predicted adaptively during contrastive training, so bands with different signal statistics are balanced automatically. On the ADFTD cohort under a strict leave-one-subject-out protocol, Delta2Gamma separates Alzheimer's disease from cognitively normal controls with 92.4\% accuracy. This exceeds both supervised backbones and recent dedicated EEG methods.

</details>


### [236] [Physics-Informed and Hybrid Machine Learning in Additive Manufacturing: Application to Fused Filament Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17246)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Sankaran Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了几种物理信息驱动和混合机器学习策略，将物理知识融入基于实验数据的深度学习模型中，用于预测熔融沉积成型（FFF）零件的粘接质量和孔隙率。通过三种策略（损失函数物理约束、物理模型输出作为额外输入、物理模型预训练）的八种组合，验证了即使实验数据有限，组合多种策略也能获得准确的机器学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFF增材制造零件的粘接质量和孔隙率预测对保证零件性能至关重要，但纯实验数据驱动的深度学习模型往往数据需求大且缺乏物理一致性，因此需要将物理知识融入数据驱动模型，以确保预测结果的物理意义和模型在有限数据下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出三种物理知识融入深度神经网络（DNN）的策略：(1) 在DNN损失函数中加入物理约束；(2) 将多物理场FFF仿真模型的输出作为DNN的额外输入特征；(3) 先用物理模型的输入-输出对DNN进行预训练，再用实验数据微调更新。研究者将这三种策略组合成八种不同的方案进行系统比较。

Result: 结果表明，将多种物理知识融入策略组合使用，能够在实验数据有限的情况下产生准确的机器学习模型，同时通过物理约束确保了粘接质量与拉伸强度之间的一致性关系，使得孔隙率预测具有物理意义。

Conclusion: 组合多种物理信息策略优于单一策略，能在有限实验数据条件下有效预测FFF零件的粘接质量和孔隙率，为物理-数据混合驱动的增材制造质量预测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This article investigates several physics-informed and hybrid machine learning strategies that incorporate physics knowledge in experimental data-driven deep-learning models for predicting the bond quality and porosity of fused filament fabrication (FFF) parts. Three types of strategies are explored to incorporate physics constraints and multi-physics FFF simulation results into a deep neural network (DNN), thus ensuring consistency with physical laws: (1) incorporate physics constraints within the loss function of the DNN, (2) use physics model outputs as additional inputs to the DNN model, and (3) pre-train a DNN model with physics model input-output and then update it with experimental data. These strategies help to enforce a physically consistent relationship between bond quality and tensile strength, thus making porosity predictions physically meaningful. Eight different combinations of the above strategies are investigated. The results show how the combination of multiple strategies produces accurate machine learning models even with limited experimental data.

</details>


### [237] [Co-RL: Unsupervised Reasoning Emerges from Diverse Cohort in Multi-agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17253)
*Yunhao Yang,Yuexin Bian,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Tianjin Huang,Yuanyuan Shi,Ziang Xiao,Nuno Vasconcelos,Yijiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Co-RL框架，通过多个不共享参数的模型进行协作式多智能体强化学习，让模型从同伴的输出中获取奖励信号，实现无监督推理能力的涌现。实验表明Co-RL在文本和多模态基准上均优于无标签方法，甚至匹配或超越有监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法在提升语言模型推理能力时严重依赖ground-truth监督（如可验证奖励），这类标注成本高昂且随着模型推理能力超越人类评估能力而愈发稀缺。自奖励RL虽减少了这种依赖，但仅依赖自生成反馈会强化已有偏见、降低响应多样性，最终导致同质化响应和训练崩溃。

Method: Co-RL框架采用多个参数完全解耦的模型进行协作式多智能体训练，每个模型的奖励信号来自同伴模型的输出而非自身。同时通过异构模型家族、模型大小和改写训练样本等方式增加群体（cohort）多样性，以减少相关错误，打破自强化反馈循环，从而提升推理性能、维持行为多样性并缓解训练崩溃。

Result: 在纯文本领域，Co-RL在七个基准上平均提升3.0-8.6%；在多模态领域，在四个基准上平均提升2.3-7.2%。Co-RL持续优于基线模型和先前的无标签方法，并匹配或超越有监督方法。

Conclusion: 协作式多智能体训练可以驱动无监督推理能力的涌现，群体多样性是缓解自强化反馈循环的关键因素。该方法无需任何ground-truth标签，在文本和视觉-语言两个领域均展现出强大的性能优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach for improving reasoning in language and vision-language models, yet its strongest successes still depend heavily on ground-truth supervision (e.g., verifiable reward). Such annotations are costly to obtain and become increasingly scarce as reasoning capabilities advance beyond what humans can reliably evaluate. Self-rewarding RL reduces this dependence by enabling models to derive reward signals from their own completions. However, training solely on self-generated feedback can reinforce existing biases and suboptimal behaviors, reduce response diversity, and ultimately lead to homogenized responses and training collapse. In this work, we show that unsupervised reasoning can emerge through cooperative multi-agent training. We introduce Co-RL, a framework in which multiple decoupled models, sharing no parameters, are simultaneously optimized through RL using rewards derived from their peers. We further show that increasing cohort diversity, through heterogeneous model families, sizes, and rephrased training samples, reduces the correlated errors that drive self-reinforcing feedback loops. This diversity consistently improves reasoning performance, maintains behavioral diversity, and mitigates training collapse. Across text-only and multimodal domains, Co-RL consistently outperforms the base models and prior label-free approaches, while matching or surpassing supervised methods, without access to any ground-truth labels. Concretely, Co-RL yields average gains of 3.0-8.6% across seven text-only benchmarks for LLMs and 2.3-7.2% across four multimodal benchmarks for VLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/DrStranded/Co-RL.

</details>


### [238] [Understanding Curriculum Learning in Large Language Models via Cross-Difficulty Optimization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17268)
*Zhikai Ding,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析不同课程学习调度引发的优化动态，提出相对迁移（Relative Transfer）作为衡量跨难度知识迁移的原则性指标，并据此设计迁移感知动态课程采样（TDCS）方法，在多个推理基准上优于代表性调度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习在大语言模型后训练中被广泛采用，但其在不同推理任务上的效果差异显著，说明不存在普遍最优的单一课程。这引出了一个根本性问题：什么决定了课程学习何时有效？现有研究缺乏对课程学习有效性的统一优化层面的解释。

Method: 首先分析不同课程调度引发的优化动态，证明不同难度层级间的迁移关系刻画了课程学习诱导的优化动态；将这一关系形式化为相对迁移（Relative Transfer）度量。基于该度量，提出迁移感知动态课程采样（TDCS），在训练过程中根据估计的迁移关系动态调整采样分布。

Result: 在多个推理基准上的大量实验表明，TDCS在不同任务、模型规模和训练范式下均持续优于代表性调度策略。

Conclusion: 该工作通过跨难度迁移提供了一个统一的基于优化的课程学习解释，回答了课程学习何时有效的问题，为后续课程调度设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Curriculum learning has been widely adopted in the post-training of large language models by organizing training data from easy to hard. However, its effectiveness varies substantially across reasoning tasks, suggesting that no single curriculum is universally optimal and raising a fundamental question: what determines when curriculum learning works? In this paper, we answer this question by analyzing the optimization dynamics induced by different curriculum schedules. We show that the transfer relationship between different difficulty levels characterizes the optimization dynamics induced by curriculum learning, which in turn explains the effectiveness of different curriculum schedules, and formalize this relationship as Relative Transfer, a principled measure of cross-difficulty knowledge transfer. Based on this measurement, we derive Transfer-aware Dynamic Curriculum Sampling (TDCS), which dynamically adjusts the sampling distribution according to the estimated transfer relationship throughout training. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TDCS consistently outperforms representative scheduling strategies across different tasks, model scales, and training paradigms. More importantly, our work provides a unified optimization-based explanation of curriculum learning through cross-difficulty transfer.

</details>


### [239] [Rethinking Irregular Time Series Forecasting from the Perspective of Basis Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17284)
*Rongwen Li,Changjian Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DNBNet（去偏神经基函数网络）用于不规则时间序列预测，通过重要性采样修正渐近偏差、用神经网络参数化基函数以适应多样时间模式，并结合多尺度分解与质量感知融合机制处理数据稀疏性，在多个真实数据集上验证了有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则时间序列（稀疏观测、非均匀采样）在医疗、气象等领域至关重要，但现有基于预定义基函数聚合观测为固定维度响应系数的方法存在两个关键局限：(i)忽略时间戳采样密度导致潜在的非消失渐近偏差；(ii)预定义基函数对不同时间模式的适应性有限。

Method: 提出DNBNet，核心为去偏神经基函数响应机制：通过重要性采样修正渐近偏差，同时用神经网络参数化基函数以自适应不同时间模式。针对不规则数据的稀疏性，设计了基于平均池化的多尺度分解模块和质量感知融合机制以获取更丰富的表征，最后采用双分支解码器进行预测。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，DNBNet在不规则时间序列预测中表现优异，具有强泛化能力，代码已开源。

Conclusion: DNBNet通过去偏机制和神经参数化基函数有效解决了现有方法在采样密度偏差和基函数适应性上的不足，为不规则时间序列预测提供了性能更强、适应性更广的解决方案。

Abstract: Irregular time series forecasting is crucial in many domains, such as healthcare and meteorological observation. However, due to the inherent characteristics of irregular time series, including sparse observations and non-uniform sampling, accurately predicting future dynamics remains challenging. In light of these two characteristics, many existing methods aggregate irregular observations into fixed-dimensional estimated response coefficients through predefined basis functions and use these coefficients as sequence representations. Nevertheless, this modeling paradigm still suffers from two key limitations: (i) a potential non-vanishing asymptotic bias caused by ignoring the sampling density of timestamps; and (ii) the limited adaptability of predefined basis functions to diverse temporal patterns. In this study, we propose a Debiased Neural Basis-Function Network (DNBNet) to address these challenges. Its core is a debiased neural basis-function response mechanism, which corrects asymptotic bias through importance sampling while parameterizing basis functions with neural networks to adapt to diverse temporal patterns. In addition, considering the sparsity of irregular data, we design a novel multi-scale decomposition module based on average pooling, together with a mass-aware fusion mechanism, to obtain richer representations. Finally, a dual-branch decoder is employed for forecasting. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of DNBNet and its strong generalizability across diverse irregular time series scenarios. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/DNBNet.

</details>


### [240] [Abra: Scaling Diffusion Image Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17286)
*Kyle Chickering,Wei-An Lin,Swayam Bhanded,Dan Saunders,Akshat Tripathi,Jiaming Song,Shyamal Buch,Xinchen Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Abra（一组受控的flow-matching transformer）在10^19至10^22 FLOPs范围内进行了文本到图像扩散模型的系统性缩放定律研究，发现扩散模型的缩放行为与语言模型一样可预测，但需要约200个图像token/参数的最优数据量（是LLM Chinchilla最优配方的10倍），且对过训练更鲁棒，建议偏向更多数据而非更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算最优的缩放定律已指导前沿语言模型的训练，但对视觉生成（特别是文本到图像扩散模型）的缩放规律仍缺乏系统性研究，尤其是在更大计算预算范围内。

Method: 使用Abra——一组受控的flow-matching transformer——在跨越三个数量级的计算预算（10^19至10^22 FLOPs）上训练，系统性地研究所数据量、模型规模与计算预算之间的缩放关系。

Result: （1）扩散模型与语言模型一样可预测地缩放，但最优数据需求约为200个图像token/参数，是LLM Chinchilla最优配方的10倍；（2）扩散模型对过训练鲁棒，应偏向更多数据而非更大模型；（3）可预测性不仅限于训练损失，还延伸到生成质量指标、最优CFG设置、表示质量以及训练曲线的通用形状。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的缩放行为具有高度可预测性且系统性异于语言模型（数据需求更大、对过训练更鲁棒），为视觉生成模型的训练资源配置提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Compute-optimal scaling laws guide the training of frontier language models yet remain largely unexplored for visual generation. We present a systematic scaling law study for text-to-image diffusion models using Abra, a controlled family of flow-matching transformers trained across three orders of magnitude worth of compute ($10^{19}$ to $10^{22}$ FLOPs), reaching significantly larger compute budgets than previous works. We demonstrate that diffusion models scale just as predictably as language models but require far more data to train optimally: compute optimality occurs at approximately $200$ image tokens per parameter, ten times the Chinchilla compute-optimal prescription for LLMs. We show that unlike language models, diffusion models are robust to overtraining and that practitioners should err on the side of more data rather than a larger model. Finally, we show that this predictability extends beyond training loss to generative quality metrics, optimal CFG settings, representation quality, and even the shape of the training curves, which collapse onto a universal form.

</details>


### [241] [Beyond MSE: Rethinking the Evaluation Metric and Benchmarking for Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17293)
*Rongwen Li,Haixin Xie,Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连续时间平方误差（CSE）作为不规则时间序列预测的新评估指标，通过重要性加权消除时间戳采样分布对模型评估的偏差，理论上证明其渐进估计误差不超过MSE，并通过系统基准实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则时间序列预测研究主要聚焦于模型设计，而评估指标鲜有研究。现有基准普遍使用MSE作为评估指标，但在不规则预测场景下，MSE不仅取决于模型预测质量，还受样本特定的时间戳采样分布影响，导致对模型连续时间预测能力的评估存在偏差。

Method: 提出连续时间平方误差（CSE）指标，通过重要性加权（importance weighting）办法消除时间戳采样分布的影响。理论上证明CSE关于连续时间风险的渐进估计误差不大于MSE。构建覆盖合成、半合成及八个真实世界数据集的系统基准，用于验证CSE的有效性并评估模型的连续时间预测性能。

Result: 实验表明CSE比MSE能更准确地恢复连续时间风险。仅依赖MSE在真实场景中可能无法充分反映模型的连续时间预测性能。

Conclusion: CSE作为一种新的评估指标，能有效消除不规则时间序列预测中采样分布导致的评估偏差，为不规则时间序列模型的连续时间预测性能提供更可靠的评估方式。

Abstract: Existing research on irregular time-series forecasting has primarily focused on model design, while evaluation metrics remain insufficiently studied. Existing benchmarks typically use mean squared error (MSE) as the evaluation metric. We show that, in irregular forecasting, MSE is determined not only by the model prediction but also by the sample-specific timestamp sampling distributions, leading to a biased assessment of the models' continuous-time predictive performance. To address this issue, we propose the Continuous-time Squared Error (CSE), which employs importance weighting to eliminate the influence of the timestamp sampling distributions. We further theoretically prove that CSE's asymptotic estimation error with respect to continuous-time risk is no greater than that of MSE. Finally, we construct a systematic benchmark covering synthetic, semi-synthetic, and eight real-world datasets to validate the effectiveness of CSE and systematically evaluate models' continuous-time predictive performance. Experiments show that CSE can recover continuous-time risk more accurately than MSE, while relying solely on MSE may not fully reflect models' continuous-time predictive performance in real-world scenarios. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/ITS-Bench.

</details>


### [242] [Agentic ESOpt: Fine-Tuning Long-Horizon LLM Agents with Minimal GPU Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17310)
*Zhi Zheng,Rongsheng Chen,Yunpeng Ba,Zhenkun Wang,Yee Whye Teh,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic ESOpt，一种基于进化策略（ES）的智能体LLM全参数微调框架，相比基于反向传播的RL在长程智能体推理中具有模型可扩展性、灵活性和长程可扩展性三大优势，并在WebArena-Lite等多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在单轮LLM微调中表现良好，但在长程智能体推理场景中面临两个关键挑战：一是基于反向传播的重训练栈使得大模型微调不切实际（显存开销大）；二是长轨迹导致信用分配（credit assignment）显著困难。作者因此论证进化策略（ES）可以成为长程LLM智能体微调的更优选择。

Method: Agentic ESOpt是一个全参数智能体微调框架，支持参数-上下文协同进化。每步对当前LLM参数施加随机扰动（perturbations），评估生成的智能体获得奖励，再进行在线奖励加权更新。此外，引入扰动尺度σ的余弦衰减调度以平衡探索与适应。框架采用轻量级黑盒反馈接口，便于与提示词空间进化（如技能优化、测试时计算）组合。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite上，对Qwen-3.5-27B进行全参数优化相比No Skill基线提升6.69%；在测试时自动化启发式设计任务中，Agentic ESOpt在36个设置里的28个中超越匹配基线。

Conclusion: 进化策略（ES）相比Agentic RL在长程LLM智能体微调中具有显著优势：只需推理级GPU内存即可实现全参数优化，黑盒接口天然支持与提示词进化组合，且通过轨迹级参数归因避免跨horizon的奖励分解难题。Agentic ESOpt验证了ES作为长程智能体微调范式的可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has been promising in single-turn LLM fine-tuning. However, long-horizon agentic reasoning introduces increasingly branching interactions and sparse rewards, exposing several limitations of RL: its heavyweight backpropagation-based training stack makes it impractical to fine-tune larger LLMs, and longer-horizon trajectories make credit assignment in RL substantially harder. This paper argues that evolution strategies (ES) can be a better choice for fine-tuning long-horizon LLM agents. Compared with agentic RL, ES offers three key advantages: 1) Model Scalability: ES enables full-parameter optimization with only minimal, inference-level GPU memory, making it possible to fine-tune large LLMs. 2) Flexibility: its lightweight, black-box feedback interface makes ES fine-tuning easy to compose with prompt-space evolution (e.g., skill optimization & test-time compute); and 3) Long-Horizon Scalability: ES performs trajectory-level parameter attribution without decomposing rewards across horizons, yielding better scalability than Agentic RL as the horizon length grows. Based on this insight, we propose Agentic ESOpt, a full-parameter agentic fine-tuning framework tailored to flexible parameter--context co-evolution. At each step, Agentic ESOpt samples perturbations around the current LLM parameters, evaluates the resulting agents with rewards, and applies an online reward-weighted update. To improve the exploration--adaptation trade-off, Agentic ESOpt further introduces a cosine decay schedule of the perturbation scale $σ$. On WebArena-Lite, full-parameter optimization of Qwen-3.5-27B improves the No Skill baseline by 6.69%. In test-time automatic heuristic design, Agentic ESOpt performs online prompt--parameter co-evolution, improving its matched baseline in 28 of 36 settings.

</details>


### [243] [MoFE: A Novel Mixture-of-Experts Framework with Fourier Neural Operators for Cryptocurrency Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17342)
*Bowen Liu,Mingming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoFE框架，将傅里叶神经算子（FNO）集成到专家混合（MoE）架构中，用于加密货币价格预测。该方法基于随机微分方程理论，将波动分解为多频分量，通过自适应FNO和卷积双域专家学习连续函数映射，以动态门控MoE机制实现跨市场状态的适应性切换，在Bitcoin数据集（2020-2025）的T+1和T+5预测中达到SOTA性能，有效缓解相位滞后问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币价格预测面临非平稳性、突发状态转换和多尺度随机依赖等固有挑战，传统深度学习模型难以捕捉复杂底层动态，常产生持续的相位滞后预测，因此需要一个能够更好建模多频动态的框架。

Method: MoFE将傅里叶神经算子（FNO）集成到专家混合（MoE）架构中，基于随机微分方程框架将加密货币波动概念化为多频分量的叠加（包括用户网络驱动的基本面增长、挖矿成本与减半机制引发的季节性波动、市场情绪驱动的混沌）。包含两个核心组件：1）自适应FNO（AFNO）和卷积双域专家，学习连续的函数到函数映射以捕捉全局频谱趋势、周期性调整和微观结构；2）基于动态门控的MoE机制，实现跨多样市场状态的自适应策略切换。

Result: 在2020年1月至2025年12月的Bitcoin数据集上，MoFE在T+1和T+5两个预测周期均达到SOTA性能。模型有效缓解了相位滞后效应，在方向准确率（DA）和信息系数（IC）上表现优越；在高保真模拟交易环境中，预测增益转化为显著的超额收益和以高Sharpe比率衡量的稳健风险调整表现。

Conclusion: MoFE通过整合FNO与MoE架构，在理论上以随机微分方程为根基，实践上实现了对加密货币多频动态的有效建模，不仅提升了预测精度，还成功缓解了相位滞后问题，并在模拟交易中验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: Forecasting cryptocurrency prices remains a formidable challenge due to inherent non-stationarity, abrupt regime shifts, and multi-scale stochastic dependencies. Conventional deep learning models often struggle to capture complex underlying dynamics, frequently resulting in persistent phase-lagged predictions. To address these limitations, we propose MoFE, a novel deep learning framework that integrates Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) within a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture. Rooted in the theoretical framework of stochastic differential equations, MoFE conceptualizes cryptocurrency volatility as a superposition of multi-frequency components, which includes user network based fundamental growth, mining costs and halving mechanism caused seasonal volatility, and market sentiment-induced chaos. Specifically, specialized adaptive FNO (AFNO) and Convolution dual-domain experts learn continuous function-to-function mappings to encapsulate global spectral trends, cyclical adjustments and microstructures, while a dynamic gating based MoE mechanism enables adaptive strategy switching across diverse market regimes. Extensive experiments on Bitcoin datasets spanning January 2020 to December 2025 demonstrate that MoFE achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in both T+1 and T+5 forecasting horizons. Notably, the model effectively mitigates the phase-lag effect, delivering superior Directional Accuracy (DA) and Information Coefficient (IC). In high-fidelity simulated trading environments, these predictive gains transfer into significant excess returns and robust risk-adjusted performance, characterized by a high Sharpe ratio.

</details>


### [244] [Tight Bounds for Data-driven Multiple Hyper-parameter Tuning with Structured Loss Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17343)
*Anh Tuan Nguyen,Viet Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入实代数几何中不变连通符号胞腔分析，取代传统孤立符号向量计数，建立了多维数据驱动算法调参的紧致伪维度上界，并提出了多机制下界框架证明上界紧致性，同时将拓扑框架推广到一般双层验证损失调参及半代数应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动算法设计将超参数调优视为统计学习问题，但由于超参数与模型性能之间存在隐式、非光滑的依赖关系，建立泛化保证十分困难。现有的分段多项式假设下的多维界在理论上较为松散，且缺乏全面的下界。

Method: 首先，利用实代数几何精细化学习理论上界，通过分析块消元过程中的不变连通符号胞腔而非孤立的符号向量，避免拓扑过度计数，从而导出更严格的样本复杂度上界。其次，提出多机制下界框架，将组合容量与代数容量解耦，通过在不同机制下构造被打碎的实例来证明上界的紧致饱和性。最后，将拓扑框架扩展至一般双层验证损失调参及更广泛的半代数应用场景。

Result: 获得了比现有结果更严格的多维数据驱动调参伪维度上界，并通过多机制下界框架证明了这些上界是紧致的，同时将方法成功推广到更一般的问题设定中。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上同时补齐了多维数据驱动调参的上界精细化与下界完备性，为数据驱动算法设计的泛化理论提供了更加紧密且完整的刻画。

Abstract: Data-driven algorithm design frames hyperparameter tuning as a statistical learning problem, but establishing generalization guarantees remains challenging due to the implicit, non-smooth dependence of model performance on hyperparameters. Existing multi-dimensional bounds under piecewise-polynomial assumptions remain theoretically loose and lack comprehensive lower bounds. We resolve this by establishing tight pseudo-dimension bounds for multi-dimensional data-driven tuning. First, we refine the learning-theoretic upper bound using real algebraic geometry; by analyzing invariant connected sign cells during block elimination rather than isolated sign vectors, we avoid topological over-counting to derive strictly sharper sample complexities. Second, we present a multi-regime lower-bound framework that disentangles combinatorial and algebraic capacities. By constructing shattered problem instances across distinct regimes, we prove our upper bounds are tightly saturated. Finally, we extend our topological framework to accommodate general bi-level validation-loss tuning and broader semi-algebraic applications.

</details>


### [245] [Repetition as Reinforcement: Enhancing Sample Efficiency via Instant Episode Repetition in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17347)
*Hoda Yamani,Yuning Xing,Koen van Rijnsoever,Bruce A. MacDonald,Henry Williams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出即时剧集重复（IER）机制，通过在高回报剧集后立即重复其动作序列来提升样本效率，并将其集成到SAC和TD3中，在MuJoCo、DeepMind Control Suite及真实机器人操控任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类学习中的重复机制——通过重温成功经验来巩固记忆和技能——启发作者探索一种新的样本效率提升方法。现有方法如Experience Replay和Self-Imitation Learning（SIL）是在训练更新阶段被动地重用过去的经验，而非直接干预数据收集过程，因此作者希望设计一种机制能够主动影响环境交互过程中的数据采集。

Method: IER是一种新颖的机制，在识别到高回报剧集后，代理会在后续固定数量的剧集中直接重复该剧集的完整动作序列，通过重新与环境交互来强化有价值的行为。与SAC和TD3这两种最先进的连续控制算法集成使用。

Result: 在MuJoCo、DeepMind Control Suite以及真实世界中的机器人机械臂动态物体平移任务上，IER相比标准基线算法和基于自我模仿学习的基线算法，均表现出更好的学习性能。

Conclusion: 这种简单机制能够有效提升连续控制任务中的学习性能，验证了从人类学习原理中提取的即时动作序列重复策略在强化学习中的实用性。

Abstract: Repetition is a fundamental mechanism in human learning, where revisiting successful experiences strengthens memory, consolidates skills, and improves future performance. Motivated by this biological principle, we introduce Instant Episode Repetition (IER), a simple and novel mechanism that improves sample efficiency by immediately repeating action sequences from successful episodes during environment interaction. Unlike conventional approaches such as Experience Replay and Self-Imitation Learning (SIL), which passively reuse past experience during training updates, IER directly influences the data collection process. Upon identifying a high-reward episode, the agent repeats its action sequence for a fixed number of subsequent episodes, reinforcing valuable behaviors through renewed interaction with the environment. We integrate IER into state-of-the-art SAC and TD3 algorithms and evaluate its effectiveness on continuous-control benchmarks, including MuJoCo, the DeepMind Control Suite, and a real-world dynamic object translation task with a robotic manipulator. Experimental results demonstrate that this simple mechanism improves learning performance over standard and self-imitation-based baselines.

</details>


### [246] [CORAM: Coherent Orthogonal Rotation for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17366)
*Xinyi Sui,Ziran Liu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CORAM方法，通过将权重矩阵按行切片、在基座模型的SVD框架下分解每个专家切片、并在对应流形上合并任务特定因子，实现比OrthoMerge更强的模型合并效果，在3B到9B规模的语言和视觉语言模型上平均提升0.25至1.35个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型合并方法（如OrthoMerge）在欧氏权重空间中进行线性算术运算，无法捕捉更新的几何结构；单个正交变换虽然可以保持权重几何，却不能改变奇异值。作者希望提出一种能保留更新几何信息且允许奇异值变化的合并方法，以提升合并后模型的性能。

Method: CORAM将每个目标权重矩阵划分为行切片，每个专家切片在对应基座模型的SVD框架下进行奇异值分解，然后在各自的流形上合并任务特定因子。合并时引入放大系数λ=κ·ĉ，其中ĉ由专家与合并更新的范数估计（对N个量级相近的专家约为√N），κ根据专家更新的离散度选择而无需评估候选合并模型。此外还包括对高更新行的切片分布策略，以及针对非目标层的残差通路。

Result: 在覆盖三个模型家族、3B至9B规模、语言和视觉语言专家共四个套件上，CORAM相比OrthoMerge提升0.25至1.35个点，并且达到或超过最强的权重空间基线方法。放大系数规则在所有评估套件上与最优扫描值的差距保持在0.72个点以内。

Conclusion: CORAM通过在流形上合并每个专家切片的SVD因子，同时结合自适应放大系数、分散切片和残差通路，有效保留了更新的几何结构，在多个模型家族和任务上显著优于现有正交合并方法。

Abstract: Merging finetuned models combines specialized capabilities without joint training or access to the original data. Most methods operate by linear arithmetic in Euclidean weight space, which cannot carry the geometry of the update. Orthogonal Model Merging (OrthoMerge) uses a single orthogonal transform for each weight matrix, but such a transform cannot change singular values. We propose CORAM, which partitions each target matrix into row slices, represents every expert slice by its singular value decomposition in the corresponding base-model SVD frame, and merges the task-specific factors on their corresponding manifolds. Because manifold averaging contracts the merged update, CORAM applies an amplification coefficient $λ=κ\hat{c}$. The scale c_hat is estimated from the expert and merged update norms and is approximately $\sqrt{N}$ for $N$ experts with comparable update magnitudes. The restoration strength kappa is selected from the dispersion of expert updates without evaluating candidate merged models. This rule remains within 0.72 points of the best swept value on all evaluated suites. CORAM also includes spread slicing to distribute highly updated rows across slices and a residual pathway for non-target layers. Across four suites covering three model families, 3B to 9B scales, and language and vision-language experts, CORAM improves over OrthoMerge by 0.25 to 1.35 points and matches or exceeds the strongest weight-space baselines.

</details>


### [247] [Pathology Transport: Optimal-Transport Explanations for Clinical Data, and When Their Heatmaps (Fail to) Localize Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17370)
*Lalit Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于最优传输的整流流（OT rectified flow）生成式解释系统，在表格肿瘤标志物上实现了无监督恶性评分（AUROC 0.91-0.93）和与监督分类器一致的无标签归因（r~0.5），但在胸片上发现从合成病变到真实病灶存在显著的泛化鸿沟——传输热图是群体级信号而非病灶定位器，重构类变体在合成病变上定位良好（pointing game 0.52）却在真实放射科标注上跌至随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型有望实现可解释的临床AI，但领域很少严格检验解释性热图是否真正定位了疾病。作者旨在回答"生成式解释热图是否真的定位疾病"这一核心问题。

Method: 在两个临床分布之间训练最优传输整流流（optimal-transport rectified flow）。在乳腺癌威斯康星数据集上生成每患者反事实样本、无监督恶性评分和无标签归因；在胸片上比较传输热图与基于重构的保身份变体的定位能力，并在合成病变与真实RSNA放射科标注上分别评估。

Result: 表格数据上AUROC达0.91（五种子0.93±0.01），与监督分类器归因相关性r~0.5，但未超越逻辑回归。胸片上传输热图仅反映群体级信号；重构变体在合成病变定位（pointing game 0.52）有效，但在真实RSNA标注上跌至随机，仅监督Grad-CAM保持高于随机。

Conclusion: 核心结论是合成到真实（synthetic-to-real）的鸿沟：标签无关的热图在合成病变上看起来令人信服，并不能证明其真实定位能力。作者贡献了可复用的最优传输生成解释方案，以及用于压力测试定位能力的受控基准。

Abstract: Generative models promise a route to explainable clinical AI: rather than probe a classifier, model the distributions of healthy and diseased patients and read explanations off the geometry between them. We build such a system - an optimal-transport rectified flow trained between two clinical distributions - and use it to ask a pointed question the field too rarely tests: do the resulting explanation heatmaps actually localize disease? On tabular tumour biomarkers (Breast Cancer Wisconsin) a single flow yields per-patient counterfactuals, an unsupervised malignancy score (AUROC 0.91; 0.93 +/- 0.01 across five seeds), and a label-free attribution that agrees with a supervised classifier (r ~ 0.5) - a compact, honest interpretability engine, though it never out-predicts logistic regression. Moving to chest X-rays, we show the transport heatmap is a population-level signal, not a localiser; a reconstruction-based, identity-preserving variant does localize synthetic lesions (pointing game 0.52), yet on real RSNA radiologist boxes it collapses to chance while only supervised Grad-CAM stays above it. The central result is a synthetic-to-real gap: label-free heatmaps that look compelling on planted lesions are not evidence of real localisation. We contribute a reusable optimal-transport recipe for generative explanations and a controlled benchmark for stress-testing whether they localize.

</details>


### [248] [Integrating Novelty and Surprise for Experience Prioritization and Exploration in Image-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17373)
*Hoda Yamani,Henry Williams,Bruce A. MacDonald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NSPER与NSPER+R两种方法，将新颖性和惊喜信号分别用于经验回放优先级排序和内在奖励，以提高基于图像的强化学习中的样本效率与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于图像的高维视觉输入强化学习场景中，传统随机或次优经验采样导致冗余更新和缓慢学习，已有优先经验回放（PER）和内在奖励分别研究但未系统结合，两者互补性尚未被充分探索。

Method: NSPER利用新颖性捕获欠表示状态、利用惊喜暴露智能体对环境的理解缺口，将两者用于经验优先级排序；NSPER+R进一步将这些信号整合为内在奖励，同时改进回放质量和探索机制。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite任务的实验中，NSPER和NSPER+R在训练效率和收敛速度方面均优于基于图像强化学习的现有方法。

Conclusion: 将新颖性和惊喜信号同时用于经验回放优先级与内在奖励，能够协同提升基于图像强化学习的样本效率，为经验回放与探索的联合优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sample efficiency is a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), particularly in image-based domains where agents must learn from high-dimensional visual inputs. Traditional sampling often relies on random or suboptimal experience selection, leading to redundant updates and slow learning. Improving efficiency requires mechanisms that prioritize informative experiences while also encouraging effective exploration. Prioritized Experience Replay (PER) addresses part of this challenge by reusing high-value transitions, while intrinsic rewards promote the exploration of novel or uncertain states. However, their integration has not been extensively studied. This paper introduces Novelty and Surprise Prioritized Experience Replay (NSPER), which uses novelty to capture underrepresented states and surprise to expose gaps in the agent's understanding of the environment. We further extend this with NSPER+R, integrating these signals as intrinsic rewards to jointly improve replay quality and exploration. Experiments on DeepMind Control Suite tasks show that NSPER and NSPER+R improve training efficiency and convergence speed compared to existing methods in image-based RL.

</details>


### [249] [GUPO: Gradient Uncertainty-aware Policy Optimization for Post-Training Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17411)
*Peizheng Guo,Jianqi Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Yun Fan,Jiahuan Zhou,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GUPO方法，通过贝叶斯框架将GRPO中的组梯度建模为随机变量，利用基于Dirichlet分布的梯度不确定性来校准各梯度在聚合时的贡献，解决组梯度冲突对策略更新的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在策略后训练中被广泛使用，但同一mini-batch内不同query产生的组梯度在直接平均聚合时可能指向冲突方向，导致策略更新效果不佳。标准GRPO将实现后的组梯度视为确定性贡献，未考虑其在聚合过程中的可靠性差异。

Method: 提出Gradient Uncertainty-Aware Policy Optimization (GUPO)：将每个组梯度视为随机变量并进行贝叶斯建模以估计其概率分布；基于Dirichlet分布推导梯度不确定性；利用该不确定性校准每个组梯度在聚合过程中的贡献权重。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的大量实验验证了GUPO的有效性，表明其相比标准GRPO能更好地处理组梯度冲突并实现更有效的策略更新。

Conclusion: GUPO通过引入梯度不确定性感知机制，为GRPO在面对组梯度冲突时提供了更可靠的聚合更新方向，在多个基准上表现出优于标准GRPO的效果。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a widely used approach for post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) for reasoning. In GRPO, the group gradients induced by different queries within the same mini-batch are directly averaged to form the policy update. However, these group gradients can point in conflicting directions. Our empirical analysis suggests that group-gradient conflicts tend to be associated with less effective policy updates, motivating the need for a reliable aggregated update direction under such conflicts. Standard GRPO aggregation treats the realized group gradients as deterministic contributions and does not account for differences in their reliability during aggregation. To address this issue, we propose Gradient Uncertainty-Aware Policy Optimization (GUPO), which models each group gradient as a random variable under a Bayesian formulation and estimates its probability distribution. GUPO then derives gradient uncertainty using a Dirichlet-based formulation and uses it to calibrate the contribution of each group gradient during aggregation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of GUPO.

</details>


### [250] [General Semantic Knowledge Infusion for Spatio-Temporal Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17440)
*Mattis thor Straten,Yannick Wolker,Steffen Strohm,Prathvish Mithare,Ralf Krestel,Matthias Renz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个时空交通预测框架，利用通用知识图谱（如Wikidata）构建传感器周围的语义子图并生成嵌入，将语义信息与传统的交通传感器图融合，为GNN提供额外的语义邻接矩阵，以增强预测准确性。与以往研究不同，本文不提出新的GNN架构，而是证明外部知识对预测精度的普遍影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的GNN在时空交通预测中仅依赖传感器邻近性或路网拓扑，缺乏对传感器周围环境（如兴趣点、行政区划、位置功能角色）的语义理解，限制了预测性能。

Method: 利用通用知识图谱（如Wikidata）创建交通传感器周围的语义子图，生成知识图谱嵌入以捕获有意义的关系；将嵌入与常规交通传感器图融合，为GNN提供额外的语义驱动的邻接矩阵，使模型能够学习物理连接之外的语义上下文。

Result: 实验表明，通用知识图谱数据与传感器网络的数据融合能够提升交通预测模型的预测准确性，外部知识提供了街道网络数据无法单独传达的补充信息。

Conclusion: 本研究证明外部知识图谱对交通预测的普遍价值，不依赖特定GNN架构，并指出该方法有望改进模型的解释性。

Abstract: Although Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have made significant advances in spatio-temporal traffic forecasting, their performance is limited when they rely solely on sensor proximity or road-network topology. This paper presents a spatio-temporal prediction framework, developed to incorporate knowledge in various forms. This framework aims to improve sensor-level, contextual understanding of the environment. A general-purpose knowledge graph (e.g., Wikidata) is used to create semantic subgraphs around traffic sensors and generate knowledge graph embeddings that capture meaningful relationships, such as nearby points of interest, administrative hierarchies, and the functional roles of locations. These embeddings are then fused with conventional traffic sensor graphs to provide additional adjacency matrices informed by semantics. This allows GNNs to learn the semantic context beyond physical connectivity. This study differs from previous research in two key ways. Firstly, rather than proposing a novel GNN architecture, it demonstrates the general impact of external knowledge on prediction accuracy. Secondly, experiments with well-established traffic forecasting approaches show that external knowledge provides additional information that street network data alone cannot convey. The results show that integrating data from general-purpose knowledge graphs and sensor networks through data fusion can enhance the prediction accuracy of traffic forecasting models, and offers a potential pathway toward improved interpretability.

</details>


### [251] [Causal Local States: Scalable Simultaneous Causal Network Inference and Forecasting for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17452)
*Jonas Braun,Fabian Fischbach,Daniel Köglmayr,Sebastian Baur,Christoph Räth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因果局部状态（CLS）框架，它同时从观测数据中推断近似Granger因果交互网络并预测系统动力学。对每个节点独立选取能使其预测模型近最优的最小邻居集合，再组合所有节点进行全系统预测。在三个难度递增的基准上，网络重建保真度高，预测性能与已知真实网络的模型相当，朝着可解释、可扩展的复杂系统预测迈出一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法预测准确但作为黑箱无法揭示驱动动态的交互关系；因果发现方法能重建交互网络但不保证推断结构支持预测；现有结合两者的方法依赖单一全局超参数（如因果阈值或固定邻域大小），无法恢复异质系统的结构。需要一个能同时满足网络重建和预测性能的框架。

Method: CLS框架为每个节点独立选择最小的邻居集合，使得基于该集合的预测模型能近最优地预测该节点；各节点的邻居选择结果组合起来构成全系统的近似Granger因果网络，并用于全系统动态预测。该方法避免了对所有节点使用统一全局超参数，而是逐节点自适应地确定邻域。基于Granger因果性作为推断交互网络的准则。

Result: 在三个难度递增的基准数据集上，CLS实现了高保真度的底层网络重建，同时其预测性能与使用真实网络作为输入的模型相当。

Conclusion: CLS证明了因果结构推断与系统预测可以同时实现且互不妥协，为可解释且可扩展的复杂系统预测提供了新方向，特别适合异质系统中节点局部结构各不相同的情况。

Abstract: Machine learning methods predict many real-world systems with remarkable accuracy, but they are typically treated as black boxes that offer no insight into which interactions drive the dynamics. Causal discovery methods reconstruct the interaction network from observational data, but without regard to whether the inferred structure supports prediction. Existing approaches combining both tasks rely on a single global hyperparameter, such as a causal threshold or a fixed neighborhood size, which cannot recover the structure of heterogeneous systems. Here we introduce causal local states (CLS), a framework that simultaneously infers an approximate Granger-causal interaction network and forecasts the system dynamics. For each node independently, we select the smallest set of neighbors that allows a predictive model to forecast the node near-optimally, and the resulting neighborhoods are then combined for a forecast of the full system. On three benchmarks of increasing difficulty, we achieve reconstruction of the underlying networks with high fidelity and forecasts on par with a model that is supplied with the true network, providing a step toward explainable and scalable forecasting of complex systems.

</details>


### [252] [Evaluating RL Explainability Methods by How Much They Help Fix Bugs in Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17524)
*Ram Rachum,Yotam Amitai,Bálint Gyevnár,Reuth Mirsky,Cameron Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalXRL，一个用于评估可解释强化学习（XRL）方法的基准，通过让LLM编码代理利用不同XRL方法诊断并修复RL智能体的故障，并以奖励信号作为最终评分依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的XRL评估方法依赖功能导向的指标（如忠实度和简洁性）以及人类导向的代理指标（如主观评分或预测准确率），这些方法无法直接反映解释是否真正有助于解决实际问题。作者主张应从解释能否有效帮助诊断和修复故障RL智能体的角度来评估XRL方法。

Method: 提出EvalXRL基准，采用（环境×故障×XRL方法）的元组进行迭代评估。LLM编码代理可与XRL方法进行闭环交互：调用方法、处理输出、形成关于故障的新假设、调整参数再次调用以验证假设。这模拟了一个简化版的科学方法流程。最终以RL智能体的奖励信号为各XRL方法打分。

Result: 该论文为初步提案，尚未给出实验结果。核心贡献在于提出首个在闭环使用场景下对多种XRL方法进行头对头比较的基准框架。

Conclusion: EvalXRL提供了一种更贴合实际应用的XRL评估范式，将评估从静态的指标对比转向动态的任务驱动诊断-修复流程，有望更真实地反映XRL方法在实践中的价值。

Abstract: This preliminary paper outlines a planned evaluation benchmark for Explainable Reinforcement Learning (XRL) methods. Current evaluations rely on functionally-grounded metrics like faithfulness and compactness, and on human-grounded proxies like subjective ratings or prediction accuracy. We suggest evaluating XRL methods by how effectively their generated explanations help to diagnose and fix malfunctioning reinforcement learning (RL) agents. We propose EvalXRL, a benchmark in which a Large Language Model (LLM) coding agent uses different XRL methods to diagnose a held-out malfunction in an RL agent, and then repair it.
  Our proposed benchmark iterates across (environment $\times$ malfunction $\times$ XRL method) tuples and uses the reward signal of the RL agents to form a final score for each XRL method. The coding agent may use the method interactively: invoke the XRL method, process its output, form new hypotheses on what is broken, and invoke the method again with parameters adjusted for testing these hypotheses. This closed-loop structure may be described as a simplified version of the scientific method. Some XRL methods provide self-evaluations that follow this pattern; we propose the first head-to-head comparison of multiple XRL methods in closed-loop usage.

</details>


### [253] [No Gaussian Required: Contrastive Inverse Dynamics for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17542)
*Jack Boylan,Chris Hokamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AC-MTM，一种用于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的反坍缩正则化方法。与依赖SIGReg强制潜在分布匹配各向同性高斯预设几何不同，AC-MTM通过训练专用的逆动力学头，利用Action-NCE对比损失迫使每个潜在转换识别产生它的动作，从而消除坍缩解。逆分支训练后丢弃，推理计算量不变。在四个标准像素控制任务上与SIGReg持平，在多目标OGBench Visual Scene任务上达到80.0%成功率（SIGReg为58.0%），提升20-24个点，且无需目标网络、停止梯度、预训练编码器或重建目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA通过学习预测未来嵌入来学习世界模型，但其目标存在恒定编码器的平凡解，所有实际系统都需要添加反坍缩机制。现有方案如LeWM中的SIGReg通过正则化强制潜在分布匹配各向同性高斯，这种"规定表示外观"的方式独立于所建模的环境，在人为主观设定瓶颈。本文认为反坍缩压力应源自转移数据本身，而非预先规定的几何分布。

Method: AC-MTM保留了LeWM的前向潜在预测目标，额外添加一个仅训练期间使用的逆动力学头。该头使用Action-NCE损失训练：每个潜在转换必须从批次中区分出产生它的那个动作，这是一个坍缩编码器必然失败的判别任务。经过理论分析，作者刻画了该方法的动作空间与可观测性假设条件。训练完成后逆分支被丢弃，测试时编码、前向预测、规划和计算量均与LeWM完全一致。

Result: 在四个标准像素控制任务（匹配规划协议）上，AC-MTM可从零稳定训练，平均性能与SIGReg相当。在更难的多目标OGBench Visual Scene任务上，AC-MTM达到80.0±2.0%成功率，而SIGReg仅58.0±2.0%，每个训练种子提升20-24个点。单次50回合随机策略运行给出52%的基线估计。该方法不需要目标网络、停止梯度、预训练编码器或重建目标。

Conclusion: 对比式逆动力学提供了一种无需分布假设的反坍缩信号，从转移数据中自带监督信息，避免了对潜在空间预设几何形状的瓶颈。该方法在标准任务上与SIGReg相当，在更难的任务上显著超越。代码已开源。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn world models by predicting future embeddings, but the objective admits a trivial solution of a constant encoder, so every practical system adds an anti-collapse mechanism (LeCun, 2022; Assran et al., 2023; Bardes et al., 2022; 2024). LeWorldModel (LeWM) prevents collapse with SIGReg, a regularizer that forces the latent distribution to match an isotropic Gaussian: the representation is stabilized by prescribing what it must look like, independently of the environment it models. We argue that the anti-collapse pressure can instead come from the transition data itself. Action-Contrastive Masked Transition Modeling (AC-MTM) keeps LeWM's forward latent-prediction objective and adds a training-only inverse-dynamics head trained with Action-NCE: each latent transition must identify the action that produced it among the other actions in the batch, a discrimination task that a collapsed encoder provably fails. The inverse branch is discarded after training, leaving test-time encoding, forward prediction, planning, and compute identical to LeWM. On four standard pixel-control tasks under a matched planning protocol, AC-MTM trains stably from scratch and matches SIGReg on average. On the harder multi-object OGBench Visual Scene task, results are consistent with the prescribed geometry becoming a bottleneck: AC-MTM reaches 80.0$\pm$2.0% success versus 58.0$\pm$2.0% for SIGReg, improving by 20-24 points in each training seed. A single 50-episode random-policy run gives a 52% baseline estimate. Contrastive inverse dynamics thus provides a distribution-free anti-collapse signal that requires no target network, stop-gradient, pretrained encoder, or reconstruction objective, and we characterize the action-space and observability assumptions under which it holds. We make our code available at https://github.com/jackboyla/action-contrastive-jepa

</details>


### [254] [Domain-Adapted Molecular Language Models for Efficient Search of Make-on-Demand Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17567)
*Henrik Wille,Luis-Finley Schütz,Felix Strieth-Kalthoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了四种分子语言模型在药物发现、有机材料和催化三大领域的六个虚拟分子库中的表示学习性能，发现原生模型嵌入在不同库间表现差异大，而分子指纹作为基线稳定且强；通过显式域适应和微调可显著提升表示质量与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练分子语言模型被广泛用作分子编码器以学习结构-性质关系，但其在预训练域之外的实际适用性尚不明确，需要系统的基准评估来揭示其跨域表现能力和局限性。

Method: 对四种分子语言模型在跨越药物发现、有机材料和催化的六个虚拟分子库上进行系统基准测试，比较原生嵌入与分子指纹的发现性能，并评估显式域适应与微调对表示性能和样本效率的影响。

Result: 原生分子语言模型嵌入在库间表现差异显著，分子指纹是稳定且强大的基线；显式域适应大幅提升表示性能，微调编码器可一致提高样本效率，多个适应后的编码器在基准任务中成为最优表示。

Conclusion: 分子表示质量高度依赖目标领域，显式自适应可提升分子基础模型的实际效用，域适应的分子表示是虚拟筛选和自驱动实验室中样本高效自适应决策的有前景策略。

Abstract: Pretrained molecular language models are increasingly used as molecular encoders for learning structure-property relationships. However, their practical suitability for molecular discovery within and beyond their pretraining domain remains unclear. Herein, we systematically benchmark four molecular language models across six virtual molecular libraries spanning drug discovery, organic materials, and catalysis. Native molecular language model embeddings show substantial variation in discovery performance across libraries, whereas molecular fingerprints provide a consistently strong and robust baseline. Consistent with a potential domain-representation mismatch, we show that explicit domain adaptation substantially improves representation performance. Fine-tuning molecular language model encoders on structures from the target virtual library consistently improves sample efficiency, with several adapted encoders emerging as the top-performing representations across the benchmark tasks. These results show that molecular representation quality depends strongly on the target domain and that explicit adaptation can improve the practical utility of molecular foundation models. More broadly, our findings establish domain-adapted molecular representations as a promising strategy for sample-efficient adaptive decision making in virtual screening and self-driving laboratories.

</details>


### [255] [OOD Detection for EEG-based Machine Learning in High-Risk Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17620)
*Philipp Bomatter,Henry Gouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对脑电图（EEG）分析的分布外（OOD）检测基准，系统评估了多种OOD检测方法，并将其性能与两个临床下游预测任务的实际影响相结合进行评估，还厘清了OOD检测与模型不确定性估计这两个常被混淆的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG机器学习模型在临床等高风险场景部署时常因分布偏移而失败，但OOD检测方法在EEG领域研究严重不足；同时现有文献通常孤立评估OOD检测性能，忽略了其对下游应用的实际影响，且常常混淆OOD检测与模型不确定性估计这两个不同的能力。

Method: 作者构建了一个EEG OOD检测基准，系统评估了多种OOD检测方法，并在两个临床下游预测任务中评估这些方法的实际价值。通过解耦OOD检测能力与模型不确定性估计能力，并探索将互补方法组合形成安全防护机制。

Result: 实验结果清晰地分离了OOD检测与模型不确定性估计的表现，揭示了当前EEG OOD检测和不确定性估计技术的现状，并证明了将两者互补的方法组合起来可以为EEG机器学习模型在实际部署中构建可靠的安全网。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了EEG领域OOD检测基准的空白，证明组合OOD检测与不确定性估计方法能够为EEG模型在真实世界应用中的安全部署提供有效保障。

Abstract: Machine learning models for electroencephalography (EEG) analysis show great promise across a wide range of applications, but their deployment in high-risk domains is hindered by their vulnerability to distribution shifts. Encountering out-of-distribution (OOD) data can lead to catastrophic, overconfident predictive failures. While OOD detection methods can mitigate these risks, they remain heavily under-explored for EEG. Moreover, evaluations in the broader literature typically evaluate OOD detection performance in isolation, ignoring their practical impact on downstream applications. To bridge this gap, we introduce a benchmark for EEG OOD detection, evaluate a broad range of methods, and furthermore evaluate their value in two clinical downstream prediction task. Our results disentangle OOD detection and model uncertainty estimation capabilities, which are frequently conflated in the literature, provide actionable insights about the current state of the art for EEG OOD detection and model uncertainty estimation, and demonstrate how complementary methods for both aspects can be combined to form a robust safety net for the deployment of EEG-based machine learning models in real-world applications.

</details>


### [256] [rl-triton: High-Performance Triton GPU Kernels for Reinforcement Learning Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17641)
*Lars Simon Zehnder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍 rl-triton，一个基于 Triton 的开源高性能 GPU 强化学习信用分配内核库。其核心贡献是提出一个统一的关联扫描框架，将七种 RL 估计算法（GAE、V-Trace、Retrace(λ)、TD(λ) 回报、折扣回报、资格迹和情节前缀和）归纳为单一的一阶线性递推，仅需 O(log T) 个并行步骤即可求解，并在大规模并行模拟场景下实现 1.6-5.70× 的全调用加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 信用分配算法（如 GAE、V-Trace 等）虽然形式各异，但本质上都依赖序列上的递推计算，在 GPU 上实现时往往缺乏统一的、可扩展的高性能原语。每个算法单独实现容易导致重复开发、低效的并行策略以及不统一的终止/截断处理。

Method: 提出了一个统一的关联扫描（associative scan）框架，将七种 RL 估计算法重铸为第一阶线性递推的实例，共享同一关联扫描算子，每个算法通过算法特定的融合 Triton 内核在芯片上构造其递推系数。同时，对关联算子进行代数验证，并显式定义终止和截断情节的处理方式。

Result: 在数千环境、短 rollouts 的大规模并行模拟场景下，七种算法在两个 GPU 上均实现 1.6-5.70× 的全调用加速（相比向量化的 torch.compile 基线），无论是否包含逐步截断处理。大多数算法在更长序列下加速比进一步增大，因为基线随 log T 增长需要更多中间 HBM 往返的扫描阶段。

Conclusion: rl-triton 通过统一关联扫描框架，证明了多种 RL 信用分配算法可以在共享算子的基础上获得显著性能提升，同时保持算法的简洁性和可扩展性，为 RL 训练中的 GPU 加速提供了开源的实用工具库。

Abstract: We present rl-triton, an open-source library of high-performance GPU kernels for reinforcement learning credit assignment, implemented in Triton. The core contribution is a unified associative scan framework that recasts seven distinct RL estimation algorithms - Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE), V-Trace, Retrace($λ$), TD($λ$) returns, discounted returns, eligibility traces, and episodic prefix sums - as instances of a single first-order linear recurrence solved in $O(\log T)$ parallel steps. All algorithms share the same associative scan operator, with algorithm-specific fused Triton kernels constructing their recurrence coefficients on-chip. We verify the associative operator algebraically and define the treatment of terminated and truncated episodes explicitly. Benchmarks show a 1.6-5.70$\times$ full-call speedup over a vectorized torch.compile baseline in the massively parallel simulation regime (thousands of environments, short rollouts). The reported range covers all seven algorithms on both GPUs, both with and without per-step truncation handling. For most algorithms, speedups increase at longer sequence lengths, as the baseline requires more scan stages as $\log T$ grows, each adding an intermediate HBM round-trip. The library is available at https://github.com/simonsays1980/rl-triton.

</details>


### [257] [Elimination Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17646)
*Mian Huang,Xueqin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出消除几何（Elimination Geometry, EG）框架，这是一个有类型、原生损失、审计导向的框架，用于研究局部最优对象何时可由共享部署规则实现。EG区分局部可解性、全局可实现性和有限样本可证性，从原始目标导出原生缺陷，并区分架构阻碍与模型近似、泛化和实现误差，为架构修复提供基于证书的指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除与压缩可能会抹除预测、推理、控制或表征所需的关键区别。作者希望系统性地研究：哪些区别被丢失、诱导缺陷是否对目标任务可见、以及改变信息、架构、动作空间或部署域能否修复该缺陷，从而在归因持久性能下限之前，要求部署契约、原生端点、竞争性解释、信息与计算预算及验证规则均被固定。

Method: EG整合了几何、优化、信息论、统计学和机器学习中的工具，构建了可积性、表征可容许性、资源约束、观测重叠和公共部署等接口。框架明确区分三类性质：局部可解性、全局可实现性、有限样本可证性。形式化结果覆盖正则、协作、奇异、组合和资源受限机制，均带有明确的前置条件和声明边界。

Result: 形式化结果覆盖正则、协作、奇异、组合及资源受限机制；应用包括稀疏模型选择、无分布预测、观测治疗策略、路由专家与检索系统以及学习评分场。可复现的合成与真实数据研究展示了证书如何指导架构修复，同时记录失败的门控和未解决案例。

Conclusion: EG框架提出"阻碍感知的学习与推理"（Obstruction-Aware Learning and Inference），将结构诊断与有限数据授权、机制匹配干预及独立验证联系起来。在将持久性能下限归因于架构之前，必须预先固定部署契约、原生端点、竞争性解释、信息与计算预算及验证规则；否则应将性能缺陷归因于模型近似、泛化或实现错误。

Abstract: This monograph develops elimination geometry (EG), a typed, native-loss, audit-oriented framework for studying when locally optimal objects can be realized by a shared deployment rule. Elimination and compression may erase distinctions required by prediction, inference, control, or representation. EG asks which distinctions are lost, whether the induced defect is visible to the declared task, and whether changing information, architecture, action space, or deployment domain can repair it. EG separates local solvability, global realizability, and finite-sample certifiability. It derives native defects from the original objective and distinguishes architecture obstruction from model approximation, generalization, and implementation error. The monograph synthesizes tools from geometry, optimization, information theory, statistics, and machine learning into interfaces for integrability, representation admissibility, resource constraints, observational overlap, and common deployment. Formal results address regular, coordination, singular, compositional, and resource-limited mechanisms with explicit antecedents and claim boundaries. Applications include sparse model selection, distribution-free prediction, observational treatment policies, routed expert and retrieval systems, and learned score fields. Obstruction-Aware Learning and Inference links structural diagnosis to finite-data authorization, mechanism-matched intervention, and independent validation. Reproducible synthetic and real-data studies illustrate how certificates can guide architecture repair while recording failed gates and unresolved cases. The framework requires the deployment contract, native endpoint, competing explanations, information and compute budgets, and validation rule to be fixed before a persistent performance floor is attributed to architecture.

</details>


### [258] [Picard Proximal Monte Carlo for Parallel Bayesian Imaging with Score-Based Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17666)
*Deliang Wei,Evan Bell,Wenhan Guo,Yifan Chen,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PiX-MC是一种基于近端Langevin动力学和Picard迭代的时间并行后验采样框架，用于贝叶斯成像逆问题，在保持重建质量的同时显著降低计算时间，在512×512×80稀疏视图CT任务上使用8个GPU可实现最高50倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯成像逆问题通常需要从高维后验分布中采样，而现有的基于分数匹配和扩散模型的方法虽然提供了表达力强的贝叶斯先验，但其采样过程本质上是顺序的，在大规模成像应用中计算开销很大。

Method: 提出PiX-MC框架，结合近端Langevin动力学（利用成像似然的高效近端算子）与Picard迭代（在离散化节点间暴露并行性，支持多GPU实现）。进一步开发了多块（multi-block）和退火（annealed）变体以提升可扩展性和采样性能，并在透明假设下建立收敛性保证。

Result: 在多种成像逆问题上的实验表明，PiX-MC大幅减少墙钟时间同时保持重建质量。在512×512×80稀疏视图CT问题上，退火多块PiX-MC使用8个GPU相对于标准Langevin采样器实现了最高50倍的运行时间加速。

Conclusion: PiX-MC通过时间并行化策略有效解决了基于扩散模型的后验采样计算瓶颈问题，适用于非log-concave后验和不完美学习分数模型等实际场景，为大规模成像逆问题的高效贝叶斯推断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bayesian imaging inverse problems often require sampling from high-dimensional posterior distributions. While recent score-based and diffusion models provide expressive Bayesian priors, their sampling procedures remain inherently sequential and computationally expensive for large-scale imaging applications. We propose PiX-MC, a time-parallel posterior sampling framework based on proximal Langevin dynamics and Picard iteration. The proximal-likelihood formulation exploits the fact that many imaging likelihoods admit efficient, problem-specific proximal operators, while Picard refinement exposes parallelism across discretization nodes and naturally supports multi-GPU implementation. To further improve practical scalability and sampling performance, we develop multi-block and annealed variants of the proposed framework. We establish convergence guarantees under transparent assumptions, accommodating non-log-concave posteriors, imperfect learned score models, multi-block implementations, and annealing schedules.
